### [1] [Fine-Tuning Generative Models for Extreme Events via CVaR-Penalized Wasserstein Gradient Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11544)
*Thejani Gamage,Hyemin Gu,Zhizhen Zhang,Ziyu Chen,Markos Katsoulakis,Luc Rey-Bellet*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出CVaR-GPA，一种无需先验知识的稳健、尾部无关的生成模型微调算法，通过学习重尾分布和捕捉极端事件来提升生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准生成模型（如GAN）使用Lipschitz传输映射会保留轻尾源分布的尾部行为，难以学习重尾分布和极端事件。现有方法通常需要事先估计目标分布的尾部特征，在实际应用中缺乏可行性。

Method: 将Lipschitz正则化的KL散度与CVaR差异惩罚项相结合，构建Wasserstein梯度流。通过Rockafellar-Uryasev表示推导CVaR的一阶变分子梯度（在经典密度公式失效处有效），定义在经验测度上的粒子算法，使用自适应时间跨度和动能停止准则。

Result: 在合成各向同性和各向异性Student-t分布、Neal漏斗分布以及真实高维Fama-French 25投资组合数据集上，相比预训练基线大幅提升了全局和尾部精度。

Conclusion: CVaR-GPA通过CVaR惩罚项恢复欠采样尾部中过早消失的速度场，实现向更重尾目标的传输，无需访问预训练模型架构或估计目标尾部特征，是一种高效稳健的尾部感知微调方法。

Abstract: We propose CVaR-penalized Generative Particle Algorithm (CVaR-GPA), a robust, tail-agnostic algorithm for fine-tuning generative models to learn heavy-tailed distributions and capture extreme events, requiring no prior knowledge or estimation of the target's tail characteristics. The method is the Wasserstein gradient flow of the Lipschitz-regularized Kullback-Leibler (KL) divergence penalized by a Conditional Value-at-Risk (CVaR) discrepancy term: the Lipschitz-regularized KL divergence enables robust learning under minimal assumptions on the target distribution, while the CVaR penalty restores the velocity that otherwise vanishes prematurely in the under-sampled tails. The penalized flow admits a bounded but non-Lipschitz velocity field. This departs from the Lipschitz transport maps of standard generators, which preserve the tail behavior of a light-tailed source, and enables transport toward heavier-tailed targets. To define this flow on empirical measures, we derive the first-variation subgradients of CVaR from its Rockafellar-Uryasev representation, valid precisely where the classical density-based formula fails. The particle algorithm CVaR-GPA fine-tunes the output samples of any pre-trained model, without access to its architecture, and runs on an adaptive time horizon set by a kinetic-energy stopping criterion rather than a preset depth. On synthetic isotropic and anisotropic Student-$t$ target distributions, Neal's funnel distribution, and the real-world high-dimensional Fama-French 25 portfolio dataset, CVaR-GPA dramatically improves global and tail accuracy on heavy-tailed targets over the pre-trained baseline.

</details>

### [2] [FarSky: Task-Aware Latent-Space Coupling for Generative Intra-Hour Solar Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11254)
*Yann Fabel,Bijan Nouri,Milon Miah,Niklas Blum,Luis F. Zarzalejo,Julia Kowalski,Robert Pitz-Paal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FarSky是一个生成式太阳辐照度预测框架，通过多任务自编码器学习天空图像的共享潜空间表示，并利用潜空间扩散模型生成未来潜状态以实现概率性辐照度预测。在Almería太阳平台的多年度ASI数据集上，FarSky在确定性和概率性预测中均取得最佳性能，预测技能最高提升11个百分点，斜坡事件检测F1分数超过60%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确的太阳辐照度预测对光伏发电并网至关重要。全天空成像仪提供高分辨率云观测，适合小时内的预测。然而现有深度学习方法多为确定性预测，对爬坡事件的预测能力有限。作者提出FarSky以克服这些局限性。

Method: FarSky由两部分组成：(1) 多任务自编码器，学习共享潜空间表示，同时用于图像重建和辐照度估计；(2) 潜空间扩散模型，以近期观测为条件生成未来潜状态，再通过解码得到辐照度预测。通过随机采样天然获得概率性预测。

Result: 在西班牙Almería太阳平台的多年度ASI数据集上，FarSky在独立测试集上优于持久性模型、端到端最先进方法和生成式预测方法，取得最佳确定性和概率性预测性能，预测技能提升最高11个百分点，斜坡事件检测F1分数超过60%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，将生成模型与任务感知的潜空间耦合相结合，能够显著提升太阳辐照度预测的准确性，尤其在爬坡事件检测方面具有明显优势，展示了生成式方法在太阳预测领域的巨大潜力。

Abstract: Accurate solar irradiance forecasting is essential for the reliable integration of photovoltaic power into modern electricity grids. All-sky imagers (ASI) provide high-resolution observations of clouds, making them well suited for intra-hour forecasting. Recent deep learning approaches have substantially improved forecast accuracy but are often limited by deterministic predictions and a reduced capability to anticipate ramp events. This work proposes FarSky, a generative forecasting framework that leverages latent-space coupling to learn task-aware representations of sky images. A multi-task autoencoder first learns a shared latent representation for image reconstruction and irradiance estimation. A latent diffusion model then generates future latent states conditioned on recent observations, from which irradiance forecasts are directly decoded. Probabilistic forecasts are inherently obtained through stochastic sampling. The framework is developed using a multi-year ASI dataset acquired at the Plataforma Solar de Almería, Spain, and evaluated on two independent test datasets against persistence, state-of-the-art end-to-end, and generative forecasting approaches. FarSky achieves the best overall deterministic and probabilistic forecasting performance, improving forecast skill by up to 11 percentage points. Furthermore, it substantially improves ramp event detection over existing methods, achieving F1-scores above 60%. These results demonstrate the potential of combining generative models with task-aware latent-space coupling for solar forecasting.

</details>


### [3] [Why AI Detection Fails for Academic Integrity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11256)
*Jonathan A. Karr,Grigorii Khvatskii,Ting Hua,Nitesh V. Chawla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究通过对照实验量化了商业AI检测器在学术诚信场景中的政策失效问题：轻量级AI润色（合规辅助）被高概率误判为不当行为（64-80%），未经修改的原稿也有9-15%被标记（非STEM远高于STEM），而经AI人工化改写后检测率极低（<4%），导致"诚实使用AI被罚、规避检测者逃脱"的倒挂现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学术机构普遍使用商业AI检测器来维护学术诚信，但检测器无法区分AI编辑与完整LLM草稿，可能将两种情况都视为不当行为。现有政策隐含假设"检测分数可区分AI辅助是否符合规范"，而这一假设缺乏实证检验，因此需要量化这种政策失败的程度。

Method: 研究采用受控实验设计：选取四个学科领域的已发表英文摘要，分为2013-2015（人类撰写代理标签）与2023-2025（含AI可能参与）两个时间段，设定tau=0.50的人类/AI代理标签阈值。设计"仅润色摘要"的轻量编辑作为合规AI辅助代理，使用Pangram和GPTZero两种检测器进行检测，并对比STEM与非STEM领域差异，分析长token密度和学术词表密度的影响，最后测试Undetectable AI人工化的规避效果。

Result: 轻量"仅润色摘要"编辑被64-80%的检测率标记；未经修改的2023-2025原稿被标记率为9-15%，非STEM领域显著高于STEM（p<0.001），高分追踪到长token和学术词表密度而非作者意图。经Undetectable AI人工化后，AI标记改写稿的检出率<4%，漏检率（FNR）>96%。诚实AI编辑面临的处罚风险高于借助人工化工具规避检测的风险。

Conclusion: 检测器分数不应作为独立的不当行为证据。该研究揭示了AI检测政策在实践中的系统性失败：既过度惩罚合规的轻量AI辅助使用，又无法有效拦截经过人工化的AI大篇幅改写，且检测结果与学科领域偏见相关。

Abstract: Institutions use commercial AI detectors for academic integrity, yet detectors cannot distinguish AI editing from full LLM drafts and may treat both as misconduct. In a controlled study of published English abstracts (four domains; 2013 to 2015 vs. 2023 to 2025), we quantify this policy failure under proxy human/AI labels at tau=0.50. Light "refine abstract only" edits, a proxy for guideline-compliant AI assistance, are flagged at 64 to 80% (Pangram/GPTZero). Unmodified 2023 to 2025 originals are flagged at 9 to 15%, with non-STEM rates far above STEM (p<0.001); elevated scores track long-token and Academic Word List density, not authorship intent alone. After Undetectable AI humanization, evasion is near-total: fewer than 4% of AI-labeled rewrites remain flagged (post-humanization detection rate <4%; FNR >96%). Honest AI-editing results in a higher sanction risk than humanizer-assisted evasion. Therefore, detector scores should not serve as standalone misconduct evidence.

</details>


### [4] [Basin: Efficient and Extensible Numerical Optimization in Rust](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11279)
*Johan Larsson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Basin是一个面向Rust编程语言的数值优化库，提供统一的接口来表述和求解最小化问题，涵盖多种求解器并原生支持约束条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数值优化是科学计算中的基本任务，广泛应用于模型拟合、仿真校准、机器学习训练和工程参数选择等场景，需要一种一致且通用的方式来表述和求解这类问题。

Method: Basin为用户提供统一、一致的接口来表述和求解数值优化问题，内置广泛的求解器目录，并对约束条件提供一等公民（first-class）支持。

Result: 该库具备跨多个科学应用领域的通用性，能够处理带约束的优化问题，并提供多种求解器选择。

Conclusion: Basin作为Rust生态中的数值优化库，通过统一API、多样求解器和完善的约束支持，降低了数值优化问题的求解门槛。

Abstract: Basin is a numerical optimization library for the Rust programming language. Numerical optimization is the task of finding the inputs that minimize a function, and it is a fundamental element across the sciences: fitting a model to data, calibrating a simulation, training a machine learning model, or choosing engineering parameters that minimize cost. Basin gives users a single, consistent way to both state and solve such problems, with a broad catalog of solvers and first-class support for constraints.

</details>


### [5] [Federated Learning for Distributed CNC Tool Wear Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11281)
*Afsana Khan,Morris Stallmann,Marcin Pietrasik,Charis Kouzinopoulos,Anna Wilbik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了联邦学习在CNC刀具磨损预测中的应用，通过将刀具轨迹分布到模拟客户端来模拟联邦学习场景，并与集中式学习和本地客户端基线进行比较，结果表明联邦学习性能接近集中式学习且显著优于本地模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刀具磨损预测对CNC加工的质量和可靠性至关重要，但实际工业环境中加工数据是分布式的，且受限于数据共享政策，传统机器学习方法难以直接应用。联邦学习为在不传输原始数据的情况下进行协作式模型训练提供了合适框架。

Method: 将刀具轨迹数据分布到模拟客户端以构建联邦学习场景，训练联邦模型并与集中式参考模型及本地客户端基线模型进行性能对比评估。

Result: 联邦学习达到了接近集中式学习的性能，并且显著优于仅使用本地数据的客户端模型。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明联邦学习能够支持分布式CNC制造环境中的协作式刀具磨损预测，在保护数据隐私的同时保持了良好的预测性能。

Abstract: Tool wear prediction is an important task in CNC machining, where accurate monitoring of tool condition supports product quality and process reliability. Machine learning methods have shown potential for this task, but their use in industrial environments is limited by the distributed nature of machining data and by restrictions on data sharing between machines, sites, or organizations. Federated learning offers a suitable framework for this setting by enabling collaborative model training without transferring raw operational data. This paper investigates federated learning for CNC tool wear prediction. Tool trajectories are distributed across simulated clients to represent a federated learning scenario. The federated models are compared against centralized references and local client baselines. Results show that federated learning achieves performance close to centralized learning and improves significantly over local client models. These findings indicate that federated learning can support collaborative tool wear prediction in distributed CNC manufacturing environments.

</details>


### [6] [Drift and Dependence: Layer-wise Information-Theoretic Bounds for Replay-Based Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11690)
*Tieliang Gong,Zhongbo Zhang,Wen Wen,Yong-Jin Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个分层的信息论框架，将连续学习（持续学习）中重放（replay）策略的泛化误差分解为两个耦合效应——有限记忆的经验代理引起的表征漂移，以及优化轨迹耦合导致的依赖项，并通过Wasserstein松弛与SGLD实例化将框架转化为可操作的诊断工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续学习需要在吸收新任务的同时不遗忘旧任务，重放是一种最有效的缓解灾难性遗忘的手段。然而，现有分析将有限记忆的经验代理效应与缓冲器反复重用导致的优化耦合效应混为一个假设层面的量，未能区分二者在不同深度上的独立影响，阻碍了对泛化行为的深入理解。

Method: 提出分层的信息论框架，将期望泛化差距分解为两部分：重放诱导的表征漂移项和优化依赖项，后者进一步分解为稳定性、可塑性、交互与残差耦合四个分量。通过Wasserstein松弛处理支持集不匹配下的漂移项，得到深度相关的漂移-灵敏度权衡；通过SGLD实例化优化项，将其化简为轨迹级对数行列式预算，揭示曲率感知的梯度对齐统计量作为在线遗忘诊断指标。

Result: 控制实验与基准实验验证了所预测的记忆缩放规律、内部漏斗（interior funnel）现象，以及梯度对齐信号与遗忘之间的关联。

Conclusion: 该框架首次在逐层粒度上分离了重放策略中记忆容量效应与优化耦合效应，提供了确定最优稳定层及在线监测任务遗忘的可操作工具，为连续学习的理论理解与算法设计提供了新的视角。

Abstract: Continual learning must absorb new tasks without erasing old ones, and replay---mixing a small buffer of past examples into current training---is among the most effective remedies for catastrophic forgetting. Yet its generalization behavior is shaped by two coupled effects that existing analyses fold into a single hypothesis-level quantity: finite memory replaces each past distribution with an empirical proxy, and repeated reuse couples the buffer, the current data, and the final hypothesis through a shared optimization trajectory. We develop a layer-wise information-theoretic framework that separates these effects at every depth. Our main result decomposes the expected generalization gap into a replay-induced representation drift and an optimization-dependence term, the latter further resolved into stability, plasticity, interaction, and residual-coupling components. Two refinements make the framework operational. A Wasserstein relaxation of the drift term, valid under support mismatch, yields a depth-dependent drift--sensitivity trade-off whose minimizer identifies which interior layer to stabilize. An SGLD instantiation of the optimization term reduces it to a trajectory-level log-determinant budget, exposing a curvature-aware gradient-alignment statistic that serves as an online diagnostic of task-wise forgetting. Controlled and benchmark experiments confirm the predicted memory scaling, the interior funnel, and the alignment signal's link to forgetting.

</details>


### [7] [Terminal Symmetry as a Decision Resource: Statewise Refinement for Anytime Verified Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11318)
*Yi Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"决策-资源"视角看待序贯构造任务中的终端对称性，并将其分解为传输-精炼-认证（transport-refine-certify）三个过程，据此设计了\method{}方法。\method{}通过状态受限的过程秩与状态依赖的残差秩的序秩交集，在保证完成性的同时达到最坏情况下的验证器查询上界，在CAD装配、Mini-Programs、精确填充装箱等任务中显著提升anytime AUC，并在1,135个GRN OOD场景中取得最低平均封顶验证器成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多序贯构造任务在完成时具有精确对称性，但其执行过程是有方向性的且依赖历史。现有方法未能将这些终端对称性系统性地转化为可复用的决策资源，因此需要一种新的视角来利用这种结构提升构造效率与验证效率。

Method: 提出transport-refine-certify框架：过程证据提供方向性，终端对应关系将结构传输到等价结果，实现状态证据在转移后精炼当前决策相关性，固定验证器负责认证执行。\method{}具体实例化为：片段固定的传输过程结构、状态受限的过程秩、接受转移后刷新的状态依赖残差秩，以及一个序秩交集（秩取小），其top-k集合恰好是两个建议前缀的并集。

Result: 在CAD装配、Mini-Programs和精确填充装箱任务中，状态级刷新分别将anytime AUC提升最多6.77、21.75和8.68个点。在来自官方GRN OOD场景的1,135个目标移除片段上，\method{}在三种规模下均取得最低的平均封顶验证器成本，优于GRN和CDGS风格规划器。状态级信号也能跨聚合器与调度器组织结构迁移。

Conclusion: 终端对称性可以作为一种可复用的决策资源用于有方向的构造过程。\method{}所体现的传输-精炼-认证分解不仅在理论上提供了前缀覆盖下的完成性保证与紧的最坏情况验证器查询界，还在实践中展示了跨多种构造任务的一致性能提升，证明了该原则的普适价值。

Abstract: Many sequential construction tasks exhibit exact symmetry at completion while their execution remains directed and history-dependent. We develop a decision-resource view of terminal symmetry: process evidence supplies directionality, terminal correspondence transports that structure across equivalent outcomes, realized-state evidence refines its current decision relevance after transitions, and a fixed verifier certifies execution. This decomposition yields transport--refine--certify. \method{} instantiates the principle with an episode-fixed transported process structure, its state-restricted process rank, a state-dependent residual rank refreshed after accepted transitions, and an ordinal rank meet whose top-$k$ set is exactly the union of the two proposal prefixes. The meet provides a completion guarantee under prefix coverage and attains the tight worst-case verifier-query bound under the corresponding prefix information model; a two-state construction predicts a strict post-transition dynamic--static separation. Across CAD assembly, Mini-Programs, and exact-fill packing, statewise refresh improves anytime AUC by up to $6.77$, $21.75$, and $8.68$ points, respectively. On 1,135 target-removal episodes from the official GRN OOD scenes, \method{} attains the lowest mean capped verifier cost at all three scales among the compared GRN and CDGS-style planners. The statewise signal also transfers across aggregation and scheduler organizations. Terminal symmetry thereby becomes a reusable decision resource for directed construction.

</details>


### [8] [MOON: Multi-Objective OrthoNormalized Updates for Multitask Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11749)
*Shiji Zhou,Kunlin Lyu,Lei Zhang,Ruodong Wang,Yifan Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对多任务学习中多目标优化（MOO）在欧几里得几何下进行梯度操纵而忽略矩阵结构的问题，本文提出MOON方法，在谱-核范数几何下进行梯度操纵并使用正交归一化更新，理论上在光滑非凸目标下达到确定性O(T^{-1/2})和随机梯度下O(T^{-1/4})的帕累托平稳性收敛率，实验表明其在优化效率和最终多任务性能上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MOO方法主要将模型参数展平为向量并在欧几里得几何下进行梯度操纵，忽略了Transformer等现代架构中参数固有的矩阵结构。作者从理论角度指出，欧几里得空间中的梯度操纵在矩阵几何下并不总能产生最陡下降方向，从而限制了优化效率。

Method: 基于矩阵值参数的陡降（steepest descent）理论，提出MOON方法：在谱-核范数（spectral-nuclear norm）几何下执行梯度操纵，并使用正交归一化（orthonormalized）后的操纵梯度进行参数更新。该方法利用矩阵结构而非简单的向量化处理。

Result: 理论方面，在光滑非凸目标下证明了平均帕累托平稳性度量在确定性设置中以O(T^{-1/2})速率收敛，在随机梯度设置下以O(T^{-1/4})速率收敛。实验方面，在多个基准任务上，MOON一致地提升了优化效率和最终多任务性能。

Conclusion: 通过利用矩阵几何而非欧几里得向量几何进行梯度操纵，MOON有效克服了现有MOO方法在大规模矩阵参数（如Transformer）上的效率瓶颈，从理论和实验两方面验证了其优越性。

Abstract: Multi-objective optimization (MOO) has demonstrated significant success in multi-task learning by mitigating task conflicts through gradient manipulation. However, most existing methods flatten model parameters into vectors and perform gradient manipulation under Euclidean geometry, thereby overlooking the matrix structure prevalent in modern architectures such as Transformers. In this paper, we show that gradient manipulation in Euclidean space does not generally yield the steepest descent direction under matrix geometry, potentially limiting optimization efficiency. Drawing from the theory of steepest descent for matrix-valued parameters, we propose MOON (Multi-Objective OrthoNormalized Updates), which performs gradient manipulation under spectral--nuclear norm geometry and uses the orthonormalized manipulated gradient for parameter updates. Theoretically, for smooth non-convex objectives, we establish convergence of the averaged Pareto-stationarity measure at rates of $\mathcal{O}(T^{-1/2})$ in the deterministic setting and $\mathcal{O}(T^{-1/4})$ under stochastic gradients. Empirical results across various benchmarks show that MOON consistently improves both optimization efficiency and final multi-task performance. Our code is available at https://github.com/KunlinLyu/MOON.

</details>


### [9] [Contextual Quality-Diversity Evolutionary Reinforcement Learning for HVAC Control in Tropical Commercial Buildings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11324)
*Tran Le Vu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CQD-ERL控制器，一种基于情境质量多样性进化强化学习的控制器，用于热带水冷冷水机组及其空气侧的监督控制。该控制器维护一个按操作情境（每日天气负载聚类）和行为描述符双索引的专业策略产品档案，由无梯度进化算子和共享回放缓冲区的软演员-评论家策略梯度算子共同填充，所有动作在执行前通过确定性安全盾过滤。控制器在代表新加坡商业建筑潜在负载、冷却塔逼近度和湿度约束的两层降阶环境中训练，并与ASHRAE Guideline 36基线进行全年回测评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的强化学习控制方法倾向于收敛到单一的标量化策略，难以应对暖通空调（HVAC）系统中多样化的运行环境和负载条件，尤其是在热带气候下冷却水系统的复杂监督控制问题。

Method: 采用质量多样性（Quality-Diversity）进化强化学习框架，维护一个按数据驱动操作情境（每日天气和负载模式聚类）与情境不变行为描述符联合索引的专业策略档案；结合无梯度进化算子与软演员-评论家（SAC）策略梯度算子，二者共享一个回放缓冲区；动作通过确定性安全盾过滤确保安全执行；在两层降阶环境中训练。

Result: 在代表新加坡商业建筑的全年度回测中，与ASHRAE Guideline 36基线进行对比评估。

Conclusion: 提出的CQD-ERL控制器通过维护情境索引的多样化策略档案，能够针对不同运行情境选择和优化控制策略，从而在热带水冷冷水机组系统的监督控制中优于传统基线方法。

Abstract: This paper proposes a contextual quality-diversity evolutionary reinforcement-learning controller, CQD-ERL, for the supervisory control of a tropical, water-cooled chiller plant and its associated air side. Rather than converging to a single scalarised policy, the controller maintains a product archive of specialised policies indexed jointly by a data- driven operating context, a cluster of daily weather and load regime, and a context-invariant behaviour descriptor, filled by a gradient-free evolutionary operator and a soft-actor-critic policy-gradient operator that share one replay buffer. Every action is filtered through a deterministic safety shield before execution. The controller is trained on a two-tier reduced-order environment representing the latent load, cooling-tower approach and humidity constraints of a Singapore commercial building, and is evaluated over a full annual backtest against an ASHRAE Guideline 36 baseline.

</details>


### [10] [Long-Horizon Forecasting of Complete Financial Statements with Forma](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11327)
*Travis L. Johnson,Jiannan Jiang,Soumyabrata Chaudhuri,Yihao Chen,Lauren Falvey,Donal O'Cofaigh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ProForma-20Q基准和Forma模型，用于联合预测1-20个季度前的完整财务报表（78个细分条目），Forma通过将报表表示为(账户,季度,值)元组集合并最大化掩码元组高斯似然，在所有对比方法（经典ML、梯度提升、时序基础模型、前沿LLM）中取得最优表现，且领先优势随预测期限拉长而扩大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作缺乏对完整财务报表的联合预测，且预测期限通常不超过一年，而贴现现金流（DCF）估值中大部分企业价值落在一年之后的窗口期内。因此需要一种能够长期、完整地预测财务报表的方法，以支持更准确的估值。

Method: Forma是一个transformer模型，将财务报表视为(账户,季度,值)元组的集合进行读取，通过最大化掩码元组高斯似然进行训练。基于过去报表和行业代码，对78个报表条目进行1-20个季度的预测。评估采用变化空间R²指标。同时支持元组接口，无需重训即可进行情景分析。

Result: Forma在所有对比方法中表现最佳，包括经典机器学习、链式梯度提升、零样本时序基础模型和前沿大语言模型。其优势随着预测期限加长而扩大，高斯预测区间覆盖率始终充足。Forma的预测几乎满足会计恒等式，且可以无显著精度损失地恢复精确一致性。将未来收入路径固定可提升报表其余部分的预测精度。

Conclusion: 专业训练的模型优于通用大模型在财务报表预测上的表现，验证了为特定领域设计的专门模型在金融预测任务中的价值。ProForma-20Q提供了可复现的基准，Forma展示了对完整财务报表进行长期联合预测的可行性，对估值和情景分析具有实用意义。

Abstract: Specialist training beats generalist scale when forecasting financial statements. To our knowledge, no prior work jointly forecasts complete financial statements beyond one year, yet in a discounted-cash-flow valuation most firm value sits past that window. We release ProForma-20Q, a reproducible benchmark for forecasting 78 statement line items 1-20 quarters ahead, for anonymized firms, from past statements and an industry code, scored by change-space $R^2$. On it, Forma, a transformer that reads statements as sets of (account, quarter, value) tuples and maximizes a masked-tuple Gaussian likelihood, beats every competitor we field: classical machine learning, chained gradient boosting, a zero-shot time-series foundation model, and frontier large language models. Its lead widens with horizon, where valuation needs accuracy most, and its Gaussian predictive intervals never under-cover. Forma's forecasts nearly satisfy accounting identities; exact coherence is recoverable at no statistically significant accuracy cost. Its tuple interface supports scenario analysis without retraining, and we show that pinning future revenue paths sharpens the rest of the statement.

</details>


### [11] [Kernel Methods for Learning Operators with Multiple Inputs and Outputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11831)
*Adrien Weihs,Chunyang Liao,Jingmin Sun,Hayden Schaeffer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通用的基于核的编码器-解码器框架用于算子学习，支持多输入多输出场景，并引入KernelMO方法族。该方法在参数化偏微分方程的五个家族上达到了具有竞争力或最优的预测精度，同时比神经算子架构和深度学习模型大幅降低了训练和推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无限维对象之间的映射（算子学习）是科学机器学习中的核心挑战。现有方法在处理多输入多输出算子学习时面临维度灾难和计算成本过高的问题，需要一种既能保持数学可处理性又能实现高效计算的通用框架。

Method: 提出一个通用的基于核的编码器-解码器算子学习框架，将观测、表示、学习和重构四个步骤分离。该框架支持多输入多输出算子学习，可处理多个不同函数空间之间的乘积映射。在此基础上，进一步专门化到多算子学习场景，提出了KernelMO核方法家族，包含互补的算子值形式和乘积空间形式两种公式化方法。理论分析表明收敛率由最具挑战性的组成逼近问题决定，而非整体问题维度。

Result: 在五个参数化偏微分方程家族上，该方法取得了具有竞争力或最先进的预测精度，同时相对于神经算子和深度学习模型显著降低了训练和推理成本。

Conclusion: 所提出的核方法框架提供了一种轻量级、高效的算子学习替代方案，在数学可处理性与计算效率之间取得了良好平衡，为科学机器学习中的多输入多输出算子学习提供了实用且可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Learning mappings between infinite-dimensional objects is a central challenge in scientific machine learning. We introduce a general kernel-based encoder-decoder framework for operator learning that separates observation, representation, learning, and reconstruction. We develop this framework for multi-input, multi-output operator learning, where operators map between products of potentially distinct function spaces. Our approximation theory shows that, although the number of inputs and outputs can increase, the convergence rate is governed by the most challenging constituent approximation problem rather than the overall problem dimension. The framework leads to practical kernel methods with closed-form training and inference, combining mathematical tractability with computational efficiency. We further specialize the approach to multiple operator learning by introducing KernelMO, a family of kernel methods with complementary operator-valued and product-space formulations. Across five families of parametric partial differential equations, the proposed methods achieve competitive or state-of-the-art predictive accuracy while reducing training and inference costs relative to neural operator architectures and deep learning based models, offering an efficient and lightweight alternative.

</details>


### [12] [Weightless Fine-Tuning: Personalizing LLMs via Logit-Space Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11342)
*Bohan Zhang,Anqi Ni,Yixin Wang,Paramveer S. Dhillon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Weightless Fine-Tuning (WFT)，一种无需权重更新的解码时训练-free方法，通过跨前缀传输算子将监督残差从训练序列传输到当前提示，近似SFT的分布效果。在三个LaMP基准上达到最优平均性能，使用不到7%的有效计算量接近SFT表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SFT虽然在将LLM适配到目标分布上是标准方法，但在个性化等场景中每个作者都需要独立的权重访问、优化、存储和重训练，成本过高。需要一个无需修改模型权重即可近似SFT分布效果的方法。

Method: WFT在解码时计算作者训练序列上的监督残差，通过从dropout诱导的交叉协方差估计得到的跨前缀传输算子，将这些残差传输到当前提示。该算子捕捉一个上下文的扰动如何传播到另一个上下文的预测，用logit空间校正替代基于梯度的参数更新。

Result: 在三个LaMP个性化基准上，WFT取得最佳平均性能，在单个任务上匹配或超过SFT，平均优于其他轻量级基线。在预算受限比较中，WFT使用不到7%的有效计算量接近SFT性能。Logit级分析显示WFT与SFT诱导的logit偏移余弦相似度为0.875，覆盖95%以上的下一个token概率质量。

Conclusion: WFT能够在不需要修改模型权重的情况下捕获监督适配的分布效果，为个性化等需要低成本适配的场景提供了一种高效且有效的替代方案。

Abstract: Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) is a standard approach for adapting LLMs to a target distribution, but in settings such as personalization, where each author requires separate weight access, optimization, storage, and retraining, its costs become prohibitive. We propose Weightless Fine-Tuning (WFT), a training-free decoding-time method that approximates the distributional effect of SFT without weight updates. WFT computes supervised residuals on an author's training sequence and transports them to the current prompt through a cross-prefix transport operator estimated from dropout-induced cross-covariance. The operator captures how a perturbation at one context propagates to predictions at another, replacing gradient-based parameter updates with logit-space corrections. On three LaMP personalization benchmarks, WFT achieves the best average performance across datasets, matches or exceeds SFT on individual tasks, and outperforms other lightweight baselines on average. In a budget-controlled comparison, WFT approaches SFT performance using less than 7% of the effective computation. Logit-level analysis shows a cosine similarity of 0.875 between the logit shifts induced by WFT and SFT over 95% of the next-token probability mass, suggesting that WFT captures the distributional effect of supervised adaptation without modifying model weights.

</details>


### [13] [Confidence Calibration of Deep Learning Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12100)
*Coby Penso*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本论文针对高可靠场景下深度模型的置信度校准问题，在标签噪声、无监督域适应和隐私保护三种现实条件下提出了校准方法，包括基于噪声模型的校准框架、噪声感知的共形预测、无目标标签的域适应校准，以及局部差分隐私共形预测框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有置信度校准方法通常假设可以访问干净的验证数据，但在实际部署中这往往不现实——标签可能含有噪声、数据存在域偏移，且涉及隐私保护。在这些条件下，标准校准方法会产生误导性的置信度估计，影响模型在安全关键应用中的可靠性。

Method: 1) 提出利用估计的噪声模型重建无噪声的置信度估计，通过建模噪声标签与干净标签分布之间的关系；2) 将该框架扩展到共形预测，估计在标签噪声下的干净符合性分数；3) 针对无监督域适应，从源域性能和域差异估计目标域准确率，实现无目标标签的校准；4) 提出局部差分隐私共形预测框架，兼顾隐私保证、计算可行性和预测可靠性。

Result: 该论文提出了一系列在标签噪声、域偏移和隐私约束条件下有效的校准方法，弥合了校准理论与安全关键应用实际部署之间的差距，为可靠的、隐私保护的和抗噪声的神经网络预测做出了贡献。

Conclusion: 本研究为三种现实场景（标签噪声、无监督域适应、隐私保护）下的置信度校准提供了系统性的解决方案，表明在这些不理想条件下仍能实现可靠的校准和不确定度量化的目标。

Abstract: In high-stakes applications, reliable confidence estimates are as important as the predictions themselves. Confidence calibration ensures that predicted probabilities reflect the likelihood of correctness, making it essential for safe deployment of deep learning models. However, existing methods typically assume access to clean validation data, which is often unrealistic due to label noise and domain shifts. This thesis develops methods for improving calibration under these conditions.
  First, we address calibration under label noise. Standard methods can produce misleading confidence estimates when labels are unreliable. We propose a framework that uses an estimated noise model to reconstruct noise-free confidence estimates by modeling the relationship between noisy and clean label distributions. We extend this approach to Conformal Prediction (CP), which provides set-valued predictions with guaranteed coverage. Our noise-aware CP method estimates clean conformity scores despite label noise, enabling reliable uncertainty quantification. Next, we study calibration in unsupervised domain adaptation, where a model trained on a labeled source domain is adapted to an unlabeled target domain. Since labeled target data are unavailable, we estimate target-domain accuracy from source performance and domain discrepancies, enabling calibration without target labels. We also consider privacy-preserving settings in which user labels and model outputs must remain protected. We propose a locally differentially private conformal prediction framework that provides valid uncertainty quantification while maintaining privacy guarantees and balancing privacy, computational feasibility, and prediction reliability.
  Our results bridge calibration theory and practical deployment in safety-critical applications, contributing to reliable, privacy-preserving, and noise-resilient neural network predictions.

</details>


### [14] [Dynamics Models for Offline Hyperparameter Selection in Real-World RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11349)
*Jordan Coblin,Han Wang,Martha White,Adam White*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次将校准模型（calibration models）应用于真实工业环境——市政水处理厂，用于离线强化学习超参数选择。实验表明该方法能在高维、非平稳传感器数据上生成逼真的长时程rollout，并恢复有意义的超参数敏感性趋势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习在真实系统部署的主要障碍是超参数选择，尤其在无模拟器且在线实验成本高昂的情况下。已有的校准模型方法仅在简单模拟环境中验证过，缺乏真实世界应用的证据。

Method: 在市政水处理厂的工业场景中，评估多种校准模型方法，包括基于Laplacian距离度量的k近邻模型，用于高维、非平稳传感器数据的nexting预测任务，支持离线超参数选择。

Result: 校准模型能生成逼真的长时程rollout，并恢复有意义的超参数敏感性趋势。进一步验证了模型对年尺度数据集的扩展性、对预训练agent微调学习率选择的支持，以及在分布偏移下的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该工作为使用离线动力学模型支持RL在真实环境中的部署提供了概念验证，同时指出了未来工作需关注的实际挑战。

Abstract: A key obstacle to deploying reinforcement learning in real-world systems is hyperparameter selection, particularly when simulators are unavailable and online experimentation is costly. Prior work has proposed calibration models trained on offline data to approximate environment dynamics and enable offline hyperparameter selection, but these methods have so far been evaluated only in simple simulated settings. In this paper, we present the first application of calibration models in a real-world industrial setting: a municipal water treatment plant. We evaluate several calibration model approaches, including a k-nearest neighbors model with a Laplacian distance metric, on high-dimensional, non-stationary sensor data for nexting prediction tasks. Our results show that these models can generate realistic long-horizon rollouts and recover meaningful hyperparameter sensitivity trends. We further examine how calibration models scale to year-long datasets, how they support the selection of fine-tuning learning rates for pre-trained agents, and how robust they are under distribution shift. Overall, our findings provide a proof of concept for using offline dynamics models to support RL deployment in real-world environments, while highlighting important practical challenges for future work.

</details>


### [15] [Market-Information-Aware Gated-LoRA of Foundation Models for Transferable Day-Ahead Electricity Price Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11359)
*Hang Fan,Wei Wei,Shengwei Mei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个市场信息感知的迁移学习框架，将Chronos-2时间序列基础模型适配到日前电价预测任务。框架通过多源市场信息（MSMI）接口对齐7天价格上下文与预清理供需、备用、维护、机组容量和联络线变量，并训练源域门控低秩适配器（LoRA），仅更新约1%的模型参数且无需目标市场标签。在中国四个省级日前现货市场的留一市场验证协议下，该框架相比市场信息感知的零样本Chronos-2平均降低MAE/RMSE 6.24%/7.99%，相比朴素Source-LoRA降低3.05%/3.52%，为数据稀缺电力市场提供了实用迁移路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电价预测对市场参与者至关重要，但价格波动大、具有市场特异性且与预期系统状态紧密相关。现有监督方法高度依赖特定市场的历史数据，在新建立或数据稀缺的市场中难以应用。本文旨在解决跨市场迁移时的数据稀缺问题，探索如何利用基础模型的迁移能力实现低资源场景下的电价预测。

Method: 提出市场信息感知适配（Market-Information-Aware Adaptation）框架：1) 构建多源市场信息（MSMI）接口，将7天价格上下文与预清理供需、备用、维护、机组容量和联络线等变量对齐；2) 在源域训练门控低秩适配器（Gated LoRA），仅更新约1%参数且无需目标市场标签；3) 门控机制根据备用裕度和运行状态信号对冻结的源适配器进行缩放；4) 采用留一市场（leave-one-market-out）协议评估跨市场迁移能力。

Result: 在中国四个省级日前现货市场实验中，该框架相比市场信息感知的零样本Chronos-2平均降低MAE/RMSE分别6.24%/7.99%，相比朴素Source-LoRA降低3.05%/3.52%。消融实验表明，增益不能通过学习全局标量或随机门初始化重现，且相对Source-LoRA的额外改进有限。

Conclusion: 市场结构化输入和状态依赖的门控LoRA为数据稀缺的电力市场提供了一条实用的迁移路径。跨市场迁移的可行性得到验证，但门控机制带来的增量收益相对有限，未来可能需要探索更复杂的门控设计或更丰富的市场特征来进一步提升迁移效果。

Abstract: Electricity price forecasting is crucial for market participants but remains difficult because prices are volatile, market-specific, and closely tied to anticipated system conditions. Existing supervised methods depend largely on market-specific historical data, limiting their use in newly established or data-scarce markets. This paper proposes a market-information-aware adaptation framework that transfers the Chronos-2 time-series foundation model to day-ahead electricity price forecasting. It first constructs a multi-source market information (MSMI) interface aligning 7-day price context with pre-clearing supply--demand, reserve, maintenance, generator-capacity, and intertie variables, and then trains a source-domain gated low-rank adapter (LoRA), updating about $1\%$ of model parameters without target-market labels. The gate scales the frozen source adapter according to reserve-tightness and operating-state signals. A leave-one-market-out protocol is adopted for evaluating cross-market transferability. Experiments on four Chinese provincial day-ahead spot markets show that the proposed framework reduces the average MAE/RMSE by $6.24\%/7.99\%$ relative to market-information-aware zero-shot Chronos-2 and by $3.05\%/3.52\%$ relative to vanilla Source-LoRA. Experiments show that the gain is not reproduced by a learned global scalar or by random gate initialization, while the additional improvement over Source-LoRA is limited. These results suggest that market-structured inputs and state-dependent gated LoRA can provide a practical transfer path for data-scarce electricity markets.

</details>


### [16] [Lifecycle-Optimal Tokenization: Vocabulary Size as a Deployment-Regime-Dependent Infrastructure Parameter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11361)
*Rima Mittal,Ankit Gubrani,Satyanarayana Kakollu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出词表大小并非固定常量，而是随部署场景（推理批量大小和推理量）变化的系统优化参数。通过形式化总部署成本模型并在两个GPU系列上实验，发现推理最优词表随批大小从32k（B=1）到524k（B≥64）发生16倍变化，生命周期最优词表与训练最优词表最多相差16倍，且最优区间内质量损失小于2% BPB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 词表大小是LLM基础设施中的基础设计选择，但通常基于惯例在训练时固定，而非基于部署分析来确定。作者希望证明成本最优词表不是常量，而是服务场景（推理批大小和推理量）的函数，从而为容量规划提供可操作的指导。

Method: 形式化总部署成本为C_lifecycle(V) = C_train(V) + λ·C_infer(V, B)，其中λ为推理量，B为服务批大小。在两种GPU家族（A10G，ridge≈117 FLOP/byte；A100，ridge≈183 FLOP/byte）上进行受控实验，覆盖从内存受限到计算受限的场景，并在1.3-2.3B模型规模下评估BPB质量指标。

Result: （1）推理最优词表随批大小从32k（B=1）到524k（B≥64+）变化16倍，由V×d unembedding矩阵读取的摊销驱动；（2）在1.3-2.3B规模下，质量（BPB）在V=65k处最优，确认词表偏好与规模相关；（3）生命周期最优词表与训练最优词表在生产部署中最多相差16倍。最优范围内的质量差异小于2% BPB。

Conclusion: 词表大小应被视为纯系统优化问题，在测量范围内不存在质量惩罚。具体建议：端侧部署（B=1）应使用V≈32k；数据中心服务（B≥64，λ≥10）应使用V≈131-262k。

Abstract: Tokenizer vocabulary size is a foundational design choice in large language model (LLM) infrastructure, yet it is typically fixed at training time based on convention rather than deployment analysis. We show that the cost-optimal vocabulary is not a constant but a function of the serving regime. We formalize total deployment cost as $C_{lifecycle}(V) = C_{train}(V) + λ\cdot C_{infer}(V, B)$, where $λ$ is inference volume and $B$ is the serving batch size. Through controlled experiments on two GPU families spanning the memory-bound to compute-bound regimes (A10G, ridge $\approx$ 117 FLOP/byte; A100, ridge $\approx$ 183 FLOP/byte), we demonstrate: (1) the inference-optimal vocabulary shifts 16x with serving batch, from 32k at $B=1$ to 524k at $B=64+$, driven by amortization of the $V \times d$ unembedding matrix read; (2) at 1.3-2.3B model scale, quality (bits per byte, BPB) is optimized at $V=65$k, confirming scale-dependent vocabulary preference; (3) the lifecycle-optimal vocabulary diverges from training-optimal by up to 16x for production deployments. Quality is approximately invariant across the optimal range ($<$2% BPB spread), making vocabulary a pure systems optimization with no quality penalty in the measured range. Our results provide actionable capacity planning guidance: on-device deployments ($B=1$) should use $V \approx 32$k; datacenter serving ($B \geq 64$, $λ\geq 10$) should use $V \approx 131$-262k.

</details>


### [17] [PAIR: Pairwise-Aware Inclusion Reweighting for Adaptive Rollout Allocation in RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11368)
*Pixel Nomand,Elena Voss,Marcus Hale,Sofia Reyes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PAIR方法，通过将rollout前缀视为顶点、pair梯度项视为边来构建对比图，利用prefix-only预测器估计正确性和剩余token成本，并通过凸优化设计选择正继续概率，在计算匹配的RLVR训练中相比最强点式分配器提升准确率+1.2/+1.4，同时比全组GRPO节省51%/52%的生成token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR（带可验证奖励的强化学习）大部分计算花费在生成大量长推理轨迹上。现有分配器根据点式难度或效用概念为prompt、rollout或token分配预算以降低成本，但论文识别出一个统计错配：未裁剪的留一法组相对分数梯度不是独立点贡献之和，而是关于rollout对的二阶U统计量。完成一个rollout会揭示其与所有其他已完成rollout的对比信息，这使得点式分配器在统计上不匹配真实目标。

Method: 提出PAIR（Pairwise-Aware Inclusion Reweighting）：1) 将短rollout前缀视为顶点，pair梯度项视为对比图的边；2) 使用prefix-only预测器估计正确性和剩余token成本；3) 通过凸优化设计在期望后缀token预算下选择正继续概率；4) 每个由完成顶点诱导的边按其联合包含概率的对数倒数进行逆加权。在条件独立on-policy rollout和未裁剪、未标准化的目标下，该估计器对完整候选pair梯度是设计无偏的。

Result: 在Qwen3-1.7B/4B上的计算匹配RLVR运行中，PAIR相比最强点式分配器平均准确率提升+1.2和+1.4，同时比全组GRPO少使用51%和52%的生成token。冻结种群估计器审计确认未加权的自适应选择存在偏差，而pair包含校正能在匹配的后缀成本下恢复完整pair目标。

Conclusion: 论文的核心贡献在于揭示了RLVR中梯度估计的pairwise（二阶）统计结构，并证明自适应端点选择带来的包含偏差可以通过pair级逆包含概率加权来校正，从而在更低的token预算下实现更高的准确率。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) spends most of its compute generating groups of long reasoning trajectories. Recent allocators reduce this cost by assigning budgets to prompts, rollouts, or tokens according to a pointwise notion of difficulty or utility. We identify a statistical mismatch: the unclipped leave-one-out group-relative score gradient is not a sum of independent point contributions, but a second-order U-statistic over pairs of rollouts. Completing one rollout therefore reveals contrast with every other completed rollout, and adaptive endpoint selection changes which pair terms are observable. We introduce PAIR (Pairwise-Aware Inclusion Reweighting), which treats short rollout prefixes as vertices and pair-gradient terms as edges of a contrast graph. A prefix-only predictor estimates correctness and remaining token cost; a convex design chooses positive continuation probabilities under an expected suffix-token budget; and each edge induced by completed vertices is inverse-weighted by its logged joint inclusion probability. Under conditionally independent on-policy rollouts and an unclipped, unstandardized objective, the resulting estimator is design-unbiased for the complete candidate-pair gradient. Across compute-matched RLVR runs on Qwen3-1.7B/4B, PAIR improves average accuracy by +1.2 and +1.4 over the strongest pointwise allocator while using 51% and 52% fewer generated tokens than full-group GRPO. A frozen-population estimator audit confirms that unweighted adaptive selection is biased, whereas pair-inclusion correction recovers the complete-pair target at matched suffix cost.

</details>


### [18] [Towards an approach to multivariate outlier detection for District Heating System data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11375)
*Rajko Turudija,Dušan Stojiljković,Milan Zdravković,Marko Ignjatović*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对区域供热系统子站的传输热能数据，结合室外环境温度，比较了Z-score（单变量基准）、马氏距离、主成分分析（PCA）、孤立森林和Hotelling T²检验等多种多元离群点检测方法，最终采用三种有效方法（PCA、孤立森林、Hotelling）的集成方法作为最终方案，以识别异常运行并降低天然气消耗和CO₂排放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在通过检测供热系统中的异常数据，揭示不规则工厂运行情况，从而识别减少中央供暖厂天然气消耗及CO₂排放的机会。

Method: 比较了五种离群点检测方法：Z-score（作为单变量基准）、马氏距离、主成分分析（PCA）、孤立森林和Hotelling T²检验。方法考虑了领域特殊情形（如零传输能量时间点不代表离网状态），并由领域专家讨论各方法结果，最终采用三种有效方法检测结果一致的集成方法。

Result: PCA、孤立森林和Hotelling T²检验方法在检测离群点方面提供了相关且有效的结果；马氏距离和Z-score方法效果不佳。集成方法（基于三种有效方法一致同意的离群点）被采纳为最终方案。

Conclusion: 多变量离群点检测结合领域知识可以有效识别供热系统的异常运行，通过集成三种互补的方法能够提高检测的可靠性，为实现节能和减排目标提供了数据驱动的支持。

Abstract: In this paper, we test different methods for multivariate detection of outliers in the data of transmitted heat energy in the selected substation of local District Heating System, by also considering outside ambient temperature, namely Z-score (univariate, as a benchmark), Mahalanobis distances, Principal Component Analysis (PCA), Isolation Forest and Hotelling's T-squared test. The overall research aims at uncovering irregular plant operation, with a wider objective of identifying the opportunities for reducing the consumption of gas in central heating plants as well as the CO2 emission. The proposed approach considers specific domain circumstances, such as irrelevance of zero transmit-ted energy timepoints as indication of off-grid plant. The outcomes of the different methods are discussed with domain experts. It was concluded that PCA, Isolation Forest and Hotelling method provide relevant results. Finally, we adopt the ensemble method (selection based on the agreement of all three methods on the detected outliers) as the final approach.

</details>


### [19] [Reoptimization Algorithms for Contextual Bandits with Knapsack Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11383)
*Zhen Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有背包约束的情境赌博机问题的新算法，提出了一种基于上置信界（UCB）的简单扩展并结合再优化技术，实现了平均遗憾值 O((ln T)^3 / T)，显著优于文献中基于再优化的动态定价问题的 O(1/√T) 界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 情境赌博机与背包问题（Contextual Bandits with Knapsack）广泛存在于现实决策场景中（如电子商务、资源分配等），其中奖励函数未知、资源容量有限。现有基于再优化技术的算法遗憾界仅为 O(1/√T)，存在显著改进空间，因此需要设计更优的算法以减少期望收益损失。

Method: 提出一种自然且简单的 UCB（Upper-Confidence-Bound）算法扩展，将情境赌博机学习与背包约束下的资源分配结合，并在每轮决策中应用再优化（re-optimization）技术，即根据更新的置信区间重新求解优化问题来做出分配决策。

Result: 所提算法实现了平均遗憾值 O((ln T)^3 / T)，其中 T 为时间水平（horizon length）。该界显著降低了文献中基于再优化方法的动态定价问题中的 O(1/√T) 遗憾界。

Conclusion: 通过结合 UCB 与再优化技术，可以在情境赌博机+背包问题中实现远比现有文献更优的遗憾上界，验证了再优化策略在提高学习效率方面的有效性。

Abstract: We study new algorithms for Contextual Bandits with Knapsack. In these problems, there are finitely many types of customers, products, and resources. Each product is made from a fixed combination of resources, and resources have finite capacity. A decision maker must assign each arriving customer one out of a set of multiple possible products. Every assignment of a customer to a product will generate a random reward, which equals an unknown linear function of customer and product features, plus a noise term. The objective is to jointly learn the mean reward function, and to make online assignments to minimize the expected revenue loss relative to an optimal policy that knows the reward function. We propose a natural and simple extension of the Upper-Confidence-Bound (UCB) family of algorithms and apply re-optimization techniques. We show that by taking advantage of re-optimization, our algorithm achieves an average regret of $O(\frac{(\ln T)^3}{T})$ where $T$ is the horizon length. Our bound significantly reduces the $O(\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}})$ bound in the literature for closely related dynamic-pricing problems that are based on re-optimization.

</details>


### [20] [Mechanism Design for Generative Engines: From Exploitation toward Win-Win Outcomes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11390)
*Chen Xu,Zitian Guo,Chenyan Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究生成式搜索引擎生态中内容提供者与平台之间的"引用战争"问题，提出基于可验证内容奖励（VCR）的平台-创作者机制，通过奖赏含有可核查事实的改写而非仅惩罚可疑改写，在三个基准上平均领先最强基线12.1个百分点，实现平台与创作者双赢。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式引擎正在重塑网络生态，引用成为注意力、归因和下游价值分配的关键机制。这造成战略张力：内容提供者有动机针对模型引用进行优化（GEO攻击），而平台需保障答案质量与可信归因。作者发现该张力可升级为"引用战争"——现有的GEO攻击能自适应地规避常规防御，产生降低文档质量并引入无依据主张的内容。因此需要研究如何设计与防御机制。

Method: 将供应商-平台交互建模为带部分监控的重复Stackelberg博弈；通过局部最佳响应分析识别引用竞争何时趋近惰性稳定结果；基于此提出VCR（Verifiable-Content Rewards）机制——平台不仅惩罚可疑改写，还奖励那些浮现可核查事实内容的改写，从而将创作者激励与答案可信度对齐。方法包括博弈论分析与多基准实验验证。

Result: 在三个基准数据集上的实验表明，VCR机制持续取得最大的净防御效用分数，平均超出最强基线12.1个百分点，并在经验等价准则下实现双赢结果。

Conclusion: VCR通过"奖励可核查内容"而非仅"惩罚可疑内容"的策略，有效地化解了生成式引擎生态中的引用战争，实现了平台答案质量与创作者激励的良性对齐，为生成式引擎生态下的内容治理提供了可行的博弈论机制设计路径。

Abstract: Generative engines are reshaping the web ecosystem by making citations a key mechanism for allocating attention, attribution, and downstream value. This creates a strategic tension: content providers are incentivized to optimize for model citation, while platforms must preserve answer quality and trustworthy attribution. We show that this tension can escalate into citation wars. In repeated simulations, state-of-the-art generative engine optimization (GEO) attacks adapt to conventional defenses by producing citation-seeking rewrites that degrade document quality and introduce unsupported claims. To study this problem, we formulate the supplier--platform interaction as a repeated Stackelberg game with partial monitoring. A local best-response analysis identifies when citation competition approaches an inert stationary outcome. Motivated by this finding, we propose a platform--creator mechanism called VCR based on verifiable-content rewards. Rather than only penalizing suspicious rewrites, the platform also credits rewrites that surface checkable factual substance, aligning creator incentives with answer trustworthiness. Experiments on three benchmarks show that VCR consistently achieves the largest Net defense-utility score, outperforming the strongest baseline by an average of 12.1 percentage points, and produces a win--win outcome under our empirical equivalence criterion.

</details>


### [21] [Unmasking Toxic Mimicry in Medical Offline Reinforcement Learning for ICU Sepsis Management via Counterfactual Clinical Audits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11410)
*Hangqi Ren,Junyi Liao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了Counterfactual Clinical Audit (CCA)框架，通过基于脓毒症治疗指南的生理扰动测试来检测离线强化学习智能体在ICU治疗决策中的Toxic Mimicry失败模式。审计显示MedDT在乳酸升高时反而降低血管加压药剂量，违背复苏指南，而HCT-RL保持生理一致性，揭示了统计拟合与临床安全之间的系统性错位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准离线强化学习评估指标（MSE和FQE）仅衡量行为模仿程度，无法检测Toxic Mimicry——即智能体复制了有害的治疗模式（如舒适护理过渡期间的治疗撤出）。这导致统计上拟合良好的模型可能在临床安全性上存在严重缺陷。

Method: 提出Counterfactual Clinical Audit (CCA)框架，基于MIMIC-III数据库，通过锚定在Surviving Sepsis Campaign (SSC)指南上的生理扰动（如乳酸水平变化）对RL智能体进行压力测试。审计对象包括Medical Decision Transformer (MedDT)和Historical Causal Transformer (HCT-RL)，后者采用Causal Action Shielding、倾向性加权和Conservative Q-Learning。

Result: CCA揭示MedDT在乳酸水平升高时反而降低血管加压药剂量，与复苏指南相矛盾；而HCT-RL能够维持与生理状态一致的治疗响应。这暴露了统计拟合度与临床安全性之间的系统性错位。

Conclusion: 反事实审计（counterfactual audits）应作为医疗强化学习模型必要的评估标准，以弥补传统统计指标无法捕获的临床安全风险。

Abstract: Offline reinforcement learning (RL) offers considerable promise for optimizing ICU treatment decisions, yet standard evaluation metrics Mean Squared Error (MSE) and Fitted Q-Evaluation (FQE) assess only behavioral imitation and cannot detect Toxic Mimicry, a failure mode in which agents replicate harmful patterns such as treatment withdrawal during comfort-care transitions. Using the MIMIC-III database, we propose the Counterfactual Clinical Audit (CCA) framework, which stress-tests RL agents through physiological perturbations anchored in Surviving Sepsis Campaign (SSC) guidelines. We audit a Medical Decision Transformer (MedDT) and a Historical Causal Transformer (HCT-RL), the latter employing Causal Action Shielding, propensity-based importance weighting, and Conservative Q-Learning. CCA reveals that MedDT paradoxically reduces vasopressor dosage as lactate escalates, contradicting resuscitation guidelines, while HCT-RL maintains physiologically consistent responses. These findings expose a systemic misalignment between statistical fit and clinical safety, supporting counterfactual audits as a necessary evaluation standard for medical RL.

</details>


### [22] [Diffusion-Based Data-Driven Assortment Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11419)
*Junyi Liao,Xiaohui Jiang,Zhengwei Tong,Ethan X. Fang,Vahid Tarokh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于引导离散扩散的模型无关（model-agnostic）商品组合优化框架，通过学习反向扩散过程进行随机搜索，并引入收益引导机制平衡探索与利用，在模型误设和高维场景下能稳健地找到近优解，且能生成多样化的高质量商品组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统商品组合优化依赖参数化选择模型（如MNL及其变体），虽然可解性好但对模型误设敏感，难以捕捉复杂客户行为；需要一个不依赖特定模型假设、可扩展且稳健的优化框架。

Method: 将商品组合表示为二元向量，通过学习的反向扩散过程进行随机搜索（避免显式组合枚举）；引入基于期望收益估计的奖励引导机制，偏置局部转移概率，在生成过程中平衡探索与利用。

Result: 实验表明该方法能持续识别高质量商品组合，在模型误设下保持稳健，在高维设置中常能恢复近最优解；同时生成式扩散结构支持产出多样化的高性能组合，超越单一确定性解。

Conclusion: 生成式建模为数据驱动的组合优化提供了可扩展且稳健的新范式，展示了在收益管理之外更广泛的组合决策问题中的应用潜力。

Abstract: Assortment optimization is a fundamental problem in revenue management, typically addressed using parametric choice models such as the multinomial logit (MNL) and its variants. While these models enable tractable formulations, their performance is sensitive to model misspecification and often struggles to capture complex customer behavior. In this paper, we propose a model-agnostic framework for assortment optimization based on guided discrete diffusion. We represent assortments as binary vectors and perform stochastic search via a learned reverse diffusion process, avoiding explicit combinatorial enumeration. To incorporate decision objectives, we introduce a reward-guided mechanism that biases local transitions using estimates of expected revenue. This allows the method to effectively balance exploration and exploitation during generation. Empirically, we show that the proposed approach consistently identifies high-quality assortments and remains robust under model misspecification, often recovering near-optimal solutions in high-dimensional settings. Moreover, the generative nature of diffusion enables the production of diverse high-performing assortments, offering flexibility beyond a single deterministic solution. These results highlight the potential of generative modeling as a scalable and robust paradigm for combinatorial optimization in data-driven decision-making.

</details>


### [23] [Analysis of Federated Aggregation under Model Poisoning and Backdoor Attacks: A Reconstructed Cross-Dataset and Cross-Architecture Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11423)
*Soumya Mazumdar,Vineet Kumar Rakesh,Tapas Samanta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对联邦聚合方法进行了系统性复现与审计：重建了包含500个单元格的评估矩阵（5种聚合方法×5个数据集×5种架构×4种条件），发现Trimmed Mean在干净条件下性能最优（宏平均准确率76.02%），Krum在对抗条件下表现最佳，并识别出BadNets指标实现和FedPARETO脚手架中的关键缺陷（TTLR指标偏差及聚合权重与预测摘要之间的潜在不一致）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦聚合方法的鲁棒性比较需要同时考虑预测性能、威胁定义、指标语义和执行溯源等多个维度，但现有评估缺乏对方法、数据集、架构和攻击条件联合覆盖的系统性比较，且存在执行日志缺失、指标误用等溯源问题，需要在统一框架下进行严格审计。

Method: 构建500单元格的评估矩阵（5种聚合方法×5个数据集×5种架构×4种条件），重建并验证454个原始运行和36个修复/重运行执行日志，处理10个仅有摘要性溯源的SVHN单元格；对BadNets指标实现和FedPARETO脚手架分别进行代码审计，并通过对21个任务对（所有方法-条件组合均有原始成功日志）的分析验证排名的稳定性。

Result: Trimmed Mean在干净条件下取得最高宏平均准确率（76.02%）和最低平均任务内排名（1.70）；Krum在sign-flipping和高斯攻击下记录到最高准确率；基于14个任务对的子集分析确认排名不变；BadNets指标审计发现所有测试输入在目标标签计数前均被触发，因此指标实为Triggered Target-Label Rate（TTLR）；FedPARETO审计发现预测摘要可能描述未损坏的本地模型而聚合权重却作用于独立损坏的更新，存在潜在不一致。

Conclusion: 研究结论应视为在记录配置下的描述性比较而非统计估计或普遍鲁棒性声明；评估矩阵中每个单元格仅有一个确定的种子，且攻击和配置的精确谱系不完整；发现的方法性能排名（Trimmed Mean最优、Krum在对抗下最佳）在所记录的配置内是稳定的。

Abstract: Robust comparisons of federated aggregation methods require joint consideration of predictive performance, threat definitions, metric semantics, and execution provenance. A 500-cell seed-1 evaluation matrix was reconstructed across five aggregation methods, five datasets, five architectures, and four recorded conditions: clean, sign-flipping, Gaussian, and BadNets. Successful execution logs were identified for 454 original runs and 36 repaired or rerun executions, whereas 10 clean SVHN cells were supported by summary-only provenance. Trimmed Mean achieved the highest clean macro-mean accuracy (76.02%) and the lowest mean within-task rank (1.70). Krum attained the highest recorded accuracy under both sign-flipping and Gaussian configurations. These relative rankings remained unchanged when analysis was restricted to 21 task pairs for which original successful logs were available for every method-condition combination. Audit of the supplied BadNets metric implementation established that every test input is triggered prior to target-label counting; consequently, the retained metric represents Triggered Target-Label Rate (TTLR) rather than a conventional target-excluding attack success rate. An audit of the supplied FedPARETO scaffold further identified a pathway in which predictive summaries may characterize an uncorrupted local model while the aggregation weight is applied to a separately corrupted update, introducing a potential discrepancy between reported predictive outcomes and the updates used for aggregation. The canonical matrix contains a single identified seed for each cell, and exact attack and configuration lineage is incomplete. Accordingly, the findings should be interpreted as descriptive comparisons within the recorded configurations and not as statistical estimates or universal claims regarding robustness.

</details>

*Nicolas Girard,Jawher Ben Abdallah,Arno Gobbin,Liuyun Duan,Sacha Lepretre*

TL;DR: Click2Poly是一个基于Florence-2视觉语言模型的人工参与（human-in-the-loop）AI助理，通过响应用户点击直接编辑建筑和墙体的矢量图层，作为QGIS插件实现，加速了地理空间应用中建筑和墙体矢量图层的生产环境手动编辑流程。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建筑和墙体的精确矢量映射对地理空间应用至关重要，但现有深度学习方法虽然能自动提取，仍需要人工进行质量控制并修复复杂案例以满足制图标准，这一手动步骤劳动强度大且耗时。

Abstract: Accurate vector mapping of buildings and walls is critical for geospatial applications but remains a labor-intensive process. While recent deep learning methods have improved automatic extraction, in order to meet cartographic standards they always require a human to perform quality control and fix complex cases in the extraction. We present Click2Poly, a human-in-the-loop AI assistant designed to speed up this manual step. Extending the Florence-2 Vision Language Model (VLM), Click2Poly responds to user clicks by editing the building or wall vector layer directly. Implemented as a QGIS plugin, Click2Poly speeds up the manual editing of building and wall vector layers in a real-world production environment.

</details>


### [25] [Three Tokens Force Exponential Feature Rank in Nonnegative Kernel Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11427)
*Vicente Opazo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了全注意力（softmax注意力）与核注意力（固定的低维sketch压缩）在表达能力上存在指数级差距。在Min-IP布尔输入任务上，核注意力在序列长度≤2时可精确求解，但对三元组序列若需误差<1/2则需要2^{Ω(m)}个特征；而密集softmax注意力仅需m维得分和常数温度即可解决相同任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解全注意力与核注意力（如线性注意力）之间的表达能力差异对于权衡效率和模型性能至关重要。现有研究表明核注意力可逼近softmax，但关于其在特定上下文长度下无法实现精确表达的下界结果仍不清晰。

Method: 1）针对Min-IP布尔输入构造精确的困难任务实例，分析三元组序列的表示需求；2）通过信息论/复杂度论证给出归一化非负核注意力所需特征数量的指数下界；3）对确定性多头多层sketch模型建立基于transcript的下界（与独立答案数线性相关、与字母表大小对数相关）。

Result: 1）核注意力在序列长度≤2可精确解决Min-IP任务；2）任何单头归一化非负核注意力在三元组序列上若误差<1/2则需2^{Ω(m)}个特征，即便允许任意有限维tokenwise值和查询相关仿射读出；3）softmax注意力仅需m维分数和常数温度即可解决；4）该结论在位置相关token映射和因果最终查询下依然成立；5）上下文长度增长时下界逼近精确的2^m特征实现。

Conclusion: 核注意力与全注意力之间在表达能力上存在指数级分离：当上下文中出现两个竞争候选时，核注意力需要指数级特征才能达到softmax的精度。同时，对有限字母表sketch模型的transcript下界表明其表达能力受答案数量与字母表大小的限制。

Abstract: Full attention exposes every token pair, whereas kernel attention compresses a sequence into a fixed-dimensional sketch. We show that this distinction becomes exponential at the first context length containing two competing candidates. On Min-IP over Boolean inputs, rank-one normalized kernel attention solves every sequence of length at most two exactly. In contrast, any single normalized nonnegative kernel-attention head that succeeds on all three-token sequences with error strictly below $1/2$ requires $2^{Ω(m)}$ features, even with arbitrary finite-dimensional tokenwise values and an arbitrary query-dependent affine readout. Dense softmax solves the same task with $m$-dimensional scores and constant temperature. The conclusion survives position-dependent token maps and a causal final query. As context length grows, the lower bound approaches the exact $2^m$-feature realization. Separately, for deterministic multihead, multilayer sketch models whose cross-token channels have finite alphabets, we prove a transcript lower bound linear in the number of independent answers and logarithmic in their alphabet size.

</details>


### [26] [AutoGrable: What Is a Good Graph for a Table?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11431)
*Tamara Cucumides,Floris Geerts*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AutoGrable，一个无需训练图模型即可为表格数据自动构建图（graph）的准则与方法。其核心思想是：在最小表格到图抽象中，每行是一个节点，基于1-WL的消息传递GNN只等价于对行进行颜色细化分区；一个好的图构造应当使不同标签的行被区分、相同标签的行不被拆分。AutoGrable据此通过标签对齐风险分数来选择最优的连接列子集，无需实际构建图或训练GNN，可廉价地贪心搜索，适用于单表和外键模式，且能判定何时不该建图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格和关系数据库本身没有图结构，应用GNN前必须手工或启发式地决定哪些实体作为节点、如何连接。现有方法要么依赖人工经验，要么需在大量候选图上训练模型逐一评估，代价高昂。作者希望给出一个无需训练图模型即可判定图构造优劣的准则，从而低成本地自动搜索最优图。

Method: 在最小table-to-graph抽象下，每行作为一个节点，消息传递GNN（受限于1-WL）只能感知row集合上的颜色细化分区。因此构造图的优劣等价于该分区是否能把不同标签的行分开、且不拆散同标签行。对于关联（incidence）构造，分区由所选列固定，建图即等于选列。AutoGrable用标签对齐风险分数评估候选列子集：该分数包含持出风险（块内最佳常量预测器的风险）和占据项罚分（衡量块被占用多稀薄）。该分数不构建图也不训练GNN，因此可对子集空间做贪心廉价搜索，同时适用于单表和外键模式。

Result: 实验表明：1）在候选图空间中，该分数能丢弃大量候选图同时保留最优者；2）在受控任务中，AutoGrable能恢复生成标签的列，且在固定预测器下优于固定、随机和任务感知的构造方法；3）在真实任务中它是唯一能在无图有帮助时选择不建图的方法。

Conclusion: 通过将图构造问题转化为基于颜色细化分区的标签对齐评分问题，AutoGrable实现了无需训练图模型的廉价图自动构造，既能高效选择最优列子集，也能识别何时不建图更好，显著优于现有固定、随机和任务感知式构造方法。

Abstract: Graph learning presupposes a graph, and tables and relational databases do not come with one. Applying a GNN to them requires deciding which entities become nodes, which of them to connect, and through which relations---a decision made by hand, by schema heuristics, or by training a model on every candidate graph and keeping the best. We give a criterion that requires no trained graph model. In the minimal table-to-graph abstraction each row is a node, so a message-passing GNN, bounded by 1-WL, sees a construction only as a partition of the rows into colour-refinement classes: a construction is good for a task when that partition separates rows with different labels and does not split rows that share one. AutoGrable turns this criterion into a construction procedure. For incidence constructions the partition is fixed by the selected columns, so building a graph reduces to choosing them, and we score a candidate subset by a label-alignment risk: the held-out risk of the best predictor constant on its blocks, penalised by an occupancy term measuring how thinly the blocks are populated. The score materialises no graph and trains no GNN, so AutoGrable can search the space of subsets greedily and cheaply, and returns the resulting grable for single tables and for foreign-key schemas alike. Our experiments show that over a space of candidate graphs the score discards a large fraction while retaining the best; that AutoGrable recovers the columns that generate the label on controlled tasks and outperforms fixed, random, and task-aware constructors on real tasks under a fixed predictor; and that it is the only method compared that can decline to build a graph when none helps.

</details>


### [27] [Adversarial Resilience of Poisson-Process Submodular Maximization over Matroids: From Robust Offline Optimization to Full-Bandit Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12134)
*Vaneet Aggarwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了一般拟阵约束下的非单调子模最大化问题，证明了Spiteful Greedy Swap泊松过程（SGS-Poisson）在任意受控值预言机下仍保持1/e（非单调）和1-1/e（单调）的极限近似比，并由此推导出全赌臂组合多臂老虎机（CMAB）算法的遗憾界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的离线子模最大化算法通常假设精确的值预言机，但在实际在线学习场景（如组合多臂老虎机）中，预言机只能提供带有噪声的估值。因此需要研究算法在受控值预言机（即带有有界加性误差的预言机）下的鲁棒性，并建立离线到在线的转化。

Method: 作者为一个不修改泊松强度、单元素交换规则或恶意丢弃步骤的SGS-Poisson算法提供了对抗鲁棒性定理。该算法在任意满足|f^(S)-f(S)|≤ξ的受控预言机下，仍能以O(kξ)的加性损失实现近似的极限近似比，并使用Õ(nk²ε⁻²)次预言机调用。随后利用该离线鲁棒结果进行离线到在线归约，得到全赌臂CMAB算法。

Result: 算法在受控预言机下能保证：非单调情形的期望值至少为(1/e-ε)OPT-O(kξ)，单调情形的期望值至少为(1-1/e-ε)OPT-O(kξ)。由此转化的CMAB算法实现1/e和1-1/e的精确极限近似-遗憾因子，遗憾界为Õ(n^{1/5}k^{4/5}T^{4/5})。

Conclusion: 本文证明了SGS-Poisson算法对受控值预言机的对抗鲁棒性，从而将离线子模最大化算法成功转化为一般拟阵约束下带子模奖励的全赌臂CMAB算法，并获得了紧致的近似-遗憾权衡。

Abstract: We study nonnegative submodular maximization subject to a general matroid when the offline algorithm is given an arbitrary controlled value oracle. Our main result is an adversarial resilience theorem for the Spiteful Greedy Swap Poisson Process (SGS-Poisson): without modifying its Poisson intensity, single-element exchange rule, or spiteful drop step, the algorithm retains limiting approximation factors $1/e$ for non-monotone objectives and $1-1/e$ for monotone objectives. More precisely, under every controlled oracle $\widehat f$ satisfying $|\widehat f(S)-f(S)|\le ξ$ for every set $S$, our implementation returns a feasible set with expected value at least $(1/e-\varepsilon)\OPT-O(kξ)$ and $(1-1/e-\varepsilon)\OPT-O(kξ)$, respectively, using $\widetilde O(nk^2\varepsilon^{-2})$ oracle calls. As a consequence, the offline-to-online reduction yields full-bandit CMAB algorithms for general matroid-constrained submodular rewards with exact limiting approximation-regret factors $1/e$ and $1-1/e$ and $\widetilde O(n^{1/5}k^{4/5}T^{4/5})$ regret.

</details>


### [28] [Variational Parameter Calibration with Physics-Aware Latent-Space Surrogates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11435)
*Qiyao Zhou,Xujia Zhu,Pierre Joli,Yu Cong,Sibo Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种物理感知的神经网络潜空间框架，用于参数化动力系统的降阶前向建模与变分参数估计。该方法通过自编码器构建可微代理模型，将物理参数映射到预测流场，并利用观测监督训练增强潜变量与系统参数的相关性，从而支持在线逆问题的求解。在两个CFD基准上的实验表明，该方法在噪声、低分辨率、随机掩蔽和分块部分观测等多种现实测量条件下均优于标准代理模型，降低了校准误差与变异性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参数化动力系统的前向与逆向建模需要代理模型既能在状态预测上保持精度，又能为参数标定提供有效信息。然而，目前缺乏一种系统的端到端可微框架，将基于深度学习的降阶代理与变分参数估计有机结合，导致重建精度高并不一定保证逆问题求解的质量。

Method: 提出基于自编码器的物理感知潜空间框架：(1) 离线训练阶段，构建从物理参数到潜表示再到预测流场的可微代理映射，并通过观测监督损失鼓励潜变量保留与系统参数相关的信息；(2) 在线逆问题求解阶段，通过代理诱导的观测算子在参数空间中进行变分估计。

Result: 在两个CFD基准上评估：仅凭重建精度不足以支撑逆向建模，缺乏端到端可微分性或物理感知会导致变分参数校准失效。定量潜空间分析表明观测监督改善了案例级别的可分性和潜表示的时间组织。在含噪、低分辨率、随机掩蔽和分块部分观测的现实设定下，所提框架整体上降低了校准误差和变异性。

Conclusion: 该方法有效弥合了深度降阶代理与变分参数估计之间的鸿沟，证明了物理感知的潜空间监督对于提升逆向建模性能和鲁棒性的重要性。

Abstract: Forward and inverse modeling of parametric dynamical systems requires surrogate models that are not only accurate for state prediction, but also informative for parameter calibration. However, a systematic end-to-end differentiable formulation for coupling deep-learning-based reduced-order surrogates with variational parameter estimation remains underdeveloped. In this work, we introduce a physics-aware neural-network-based latent-space framework for reduced-order forward modeling and variational parameter estimation. The proposed autoencoder-based approach yields a differentiable surrogate that maps physical parameters to predicted flow fields through a latent representation. The observable supervision is used during offline training to encourage the latent variables to retain information correlated with system parameters, while the online inverse problem is solved in the parameter space through the surrogate-induced observation operator. The method is evaluated on two computational-fluid-dynamics benchmarks. The results show that reconstruction accuracy alone is insufficient for inverse modeling, owing to the lack of end-to-end differentiability or physics awareness for variational parameter calibration. Quantitative latent-space analysis further shows that observable supervision improves case-level separability and temporal organization of latent representations. Experiments with realistic measurement settings, including noisy, low-resolution, randomly masked, and block-wise partial observations, demonstrate the robustness of the proposed framework and show that it generally reduces calibration error and variability compared with the standard surrogate models.

</details>


### [29] [XGBoost "is all you need": the case of forecasting transmitted heat energy in District Heating Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11446)
*Milan Zdravković*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文比较了XGBoost和LSTM在区域供热系统(DHS)传输热能预测中的表现，发现XGBoost在此任务中始终优于LSTM，并且具有更低的计算成本和更小的碳足迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在时间序列预测中，传统机器学习算法在哪些场景下优于深度学习网络，以及由此带来的计算成本和环境影响方面的收益。这对于能源系统领域的数据分析任务具有实际意义，因为降低计算需求不仅能节约成本，还能减少碳排放。

Method: 基于真实世界区域供热系统(DHS)数据集，对XGBoost（传统机器学习代表性算法）和LSTM（深度学习代表性网络）两种方法进行对比实验，通过误差分布分析来阐释两种方法在不同数据区间的性能差异。

Result: 实验结果表明XGBoost在该传输热能预测任务中始终优于LSTM。误差分布分析显示，LSTM在数据可用性较少的区间会产生更显著的误差，这是其整体表现不如XGBoost的主要原因。此外，XGBoost在计算开销方面具有明显优势。

Conclusion: 在特定的时间序列预测任务（如DHS热能预测）中，传统机器学习方法（XGBoost）可以超越深度学习模型（LSTM），并且在资源效率与环境可持续性方面更具优势。这提示在选择预测模型时，不应盲目追求深度学习，而应结合任务特性和资源约束进行综合考量。

Abstract: This paper presents a comparative study of two distinct approaches, XGBoost and Long-Short Term Memory (LSTM), for forecasting transmitted heat energy in District Heating Systems (DHS). The objective is to explore scenarios in which conventional ML algorithms demonstrate better performance over deep learning networks in time series forecasting and the associated benefits in terms of computational cost and environmental impact. The study focuses on a real-world DHS dataset. Through experimentation and analysis, it is demonstrated that XGBoost consistently outperforms LSTM in this specific forecasting task. The difference is explained by the error distribution illustrating that LSTM makes more significant errors in the intervals of less data availability. The reduced computational demands of conventional ML approaches not only result in cost savings but also minimize the carbon footprint associated with data analysis tasks in energy systems.

</details>


### [30] [PAC-Bayes Beyond Parameter Space: Behavioral Equivalence, Z-Information, and Exact Complexity Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11465)
*Vasant G. Honavar,Satish Kumar Keshri,Neil Ashtekar,Zehao Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从行为等价性的视角重新审视PAC-Bayes理论，通过测度分解将经典KL散度精确分解为行为选择项和实现级项，并定义了Z信息量来量化预测行为不确定性之外的实现冗余复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典PAC-Bayes理论用KL散度衡量先验和后验分布间的差异，但预测风险只依赖于假设产生的预测行为，而非具体的内部实现。在过参数化系统中，大量不同配置对应相同的预测行为，经典KL散度无法区分行为不确定性与实现冗余，这导致PAC-Bayes复杂度被过度刻画。

Method: 通过可测行为图形式化行为等价关系，利用测度分解将配置空间上的概率测度分解为预测行为上的分布和各行为纤维上的条件分布，从而将经典KL散度精确分解为行为选择项和纤维内条件KL的期望（实现级项）。进一步通过变分刻画证明行为选择项是诱导相同行为分布的所有后验中KL散度的最小值，由纤维对称化的典型代表达到。

Result: 获得了经典PAC-Bayes KL散度的精确结构分解；定义了Z信息量（实现级贡献的负值）作为行为不确定性与KL散度之间的精确差距；证明了行为选择项的最小KL变分刻画；并展示了对称性、保持行为的扰动方向、纤维几何等概念都自然地从行为图结构中涌现。

Conclusion: 预测行为应被视为PAC-Bayes复杂度的自然研究对象。经典KL散度混合了行为层面的不确定性与实现层面的冗余，而Z信息量精确量化了这一分离，为过参数化场景下的泛化分析提供了更精细的理论工具。

Abstract: PAC-Bayes theory provides generalization guarantees by controlling the Kullback--Leibler (KL) divergence between posterior and prior distributions over a chosen hypothesis representation. However, predictive risk depends only on the predictive behavior induced by a hypothesis, not on the particular internal realization that implements that behavior. In over-parameterized systems, many distinct configurations induce identical predictive behavior, yet the classical PAC-Bayes KL divergence does not distinguish uncertainty over predictive behavior from variation among behaviorally equivalent realizations.
  We show that this distinction induces an exact structural decomposition of classical PAC-Bayes complexity. We formalize behavioral equivalence through a measurable behavior map and use measure disintegration to decompose probability measures on the configuration space into a distribution over predictive behaviors and conditional distributions over behavioral fibers. This yields an exact decomposition of the classical PAC-Bayes KL divergence into a behavior-selection term and a realization-level term given by an expected conditional KL within fibers.
  We define Z-information as the negative of this realization-level contribution: the exact gap between the KL divergence and the complexity of uncertainty over predictive behavior alone. We further show that the behavior-selection term admits an exact variational characterization: it is the minimum KL divergence among all posteriors inducing the same distribution over predictive behaviors, attained by a canonical fiber-symmetrized representative.
  Finally, we show that symmetry, behavior-preserving directions, fiber geometry, and invariance under fiber-preserving perturbations arise naturally from the same behavior-map structure. Together, these results identify predictive behavior as the natural object of PAC-Bayes complexity.

</details>
