In this work, three prompt-specialized agent roles operate under a version-controlled specification that the agents themselves authored and revised, while humans hold a small number of gates. The arrangement is kept safe by an exact verification oracle inherited from the domain, and the boundary of safe delegation lies exactly where that oracle stops seeing.
  We apply the proposed workflow in a case study, converting the two-electron-integral routines of GAMESS (General Atomic and Molecular Electronic Structure System), a mature quantum-chemistry package with a 48-year development history, from fixed-form Fortran 77 to free-form Fortran 2008. The scope of this work was twelve source files, 56,448 lines, and 225 subroutines for computing electron repulsion integrals. The agents ran as three Claude Code roles in isolated worktrees, and the work spanned four Claude model generations. Because the GAMESS group ships a standard test suite whose printed energies its user community treats as canonical, we could adopt bit-for-bit reproduction of those energies as the merge criterion, where a deviation in the twelfth decimal place counts as a failure rather than drift. All twelve source files pass a 51-test validation battery comprising the 49 standard GAMESS tests and two additional calculations, and across 612 test runs the number of chemistry-relevant differences is zero, and every file also passes the Jenkins tests that are used for continuous integration.

</details>


### [172] [VAKRA: Evaluating Multi-Hop Reasoning Across APIs and Retrieval Under Tool-Use Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12282)
*Ankita Rajaram Naik,Anupama Murthi,Benjamin Elder,Siyu Huo,Raavi Gupta,Abhinav Jain,Praveen Venkateswaran,Abdulhamid Adebayo,Danish Contractor*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出VAKRA基准，包含62个领域超过8000个可执行API，通过三个难度递增的设置评估企业级代理在结构化API与文档检索上的综合推理能力，发现即使最优模型在组合式API任务上准确率也仅有50-51%，且失败主要集中在语言中介的推理环节（实体消歧、跨源对齐）而非工具调用机制本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业环境中部署的代理需要在结构化API和文档集合之间进行综合推理，但现有基准将这些能力割裂开来评估，缺乏能够同时考察API交互与知识检索的综合评测标准。

Method: 构建包含超过8000个跨62个领域可执行API的基准，设计三个难度递增的设置：多样化的API交互风格、结构化API上的多跳推理、带自然语言工具使用策略约束的多源推理。正确性通过重新执行预测的工具调用与实时API对比验证，并兼容多种有效路径。使用固定ReAct框架来隔离模型能力与代理架构的影响，评估前沿与开源权重模型。

Result: 最佳模型在单跳端点式任务上仅达70.4%准确率，在组合式API任务上降至50-51%；性能随推理深度增加下降超过50%；策略约束问题暴露严重缺陷（不可回答查询上最低仅2.4%）。追踪分析表明失败集中在语言中介的推理环节——实体消歧和跨源对齐，而非工具调用机制本身。

Conclusion: 当前模型在企业级API+知识检索代理场景下表现仍远不充分，特别是在组合式推理和策略约束下的可靠性存在严重瓶颈；核心问题在于语言层面的推理能力而非工具使用技能，为后续研究指明了改进方向。

Abstract: Agents deployed in enterprise settings must reason across structured APIs and document collections, yet existing benchmarks evaluate these capabilities in isolation. We introduce VAKRA (e\textbf{V}aluating \textbf{A}PI and \textbf{K}nowledge \textbf{R}etrieval \textbf{A}gents), a benchmark of over $8{,}000$ executable APIs across $62$ domains with tasks spanning three settings of increasing difficulty: diverse API interaction styles, multi-hop reasoning over structured APIs, and multi-source reasoning with natural-language tool-use policy constraints. Correctness is verified by re-executing predicted tool calls against live APIs, accommodating multiple valid paths. Using a fixed ReAct harness to isolate model capabilities from agent architecture, we evaluate frontier and open-weight models and find that even the best model achieves only 70.4\% on single-hop endpoint-style tasks and drops to 50--51\% on compositional APIs; performance degrades by over 50\% as reasoning depth increases, and policy-constrained questions expose severe failures (as low as 2.4\% on unanswerable queries). Trace analysis shows failures concentrate at language-mediated reasoning - entity disambiguation, cross-source grounding, rather than tool invocation mechanics. Code is available https://github.com/IBM/VAKRA. Dataset is available https://huggingface.co/datasets/ibm-research/VAKRA

</details>


### [173] [Constructing Dynamic Master Logic Models as Knowledge Graphs for Complex System Diagnostics Using Retrieval-Augmented Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12304)
*Saman Marandi,Yu-Shu Hu,Mohammad Modarres*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种自动化框架，利用检索增强生成和大语言模型，从系统描述中自动构建动态主逻辑（DML）模型并表示为知识图谱（KG-DML），并通过在退役沸水反应堆的低压冷却剂注入系统上的应用验证了该方法的可行性与一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DML为系统行为提供了分层建模框架，但传统DML构建依赖专家对技术文档的人工解读，难以扩展到复杂系统，亟需自动化构建方法。

Method: 采用检索增强生成（RAG）和大语言模型（LLMs）作为工具，沿DML层级进行定向检索与构建，在保留功能依赖和显式逻辑关系的同时，将系统描述自动转化为知识图谱表示的DML模型，并设计多层验证方法评估各层的精确率、召回率、逻辑门一致性和整体结构完整性。

Result: 将该框架应用于退役沸水堆的低压冷却剂注入系统，多次重复运行显示构建结果具有一致性，验证了自动化KG-DML构建的可靠性和可扩展性。

Conclusion: 自动化KG-DML构建能够将技术文档转化为可执行的函数模型，支撑诊断推理、安全评估、向上故障传播和向下依赖追踪等可靠性分析任务。

Abstract: Dynamic Master Logic (DML) provides a hierarchical framework for representing system behavior by linking functional objectives to underlying structural elements. However, DML construction typically relies on expert interpretation of technical documentation, limiting scalability for complex systems. This study presents a framework for automated construction of DML models from system descriptions and their representation as Knowledge Graphs (KG-DML), using Retrieval-Augmented Generation and Large Language Models as enabling tools. Building on prior work with small-scale systems, the framework extends automated KG-DML construction and evaluation to substantially larger and more complex systems. Model construction proceeds across the DML hierarchy using targeted retrieval while preserving functional dependencies and explicit logical relationships. The resulting KG-DML supports diagnostic reasoning, safety assessment, upward failure propagation, and downward dependency tracing. A multi-level validation methodology evaluates layer-specific precision and recall, logical gate consistency, and overall structural integrity. Application to the Low-Pressure Coolant Injection system of a decommissioned Boiling Water Reactor demonstrates consistent reconstruction across repeated runs. The results show that automated KG-DML construction can transform technical documentation into executable functional models for diagnostic and reliability analysis.

</details>


### [174] [Reproducing and Stress-Testing Two Approaches to LLM Reasoning Reliability: Test-Time Probability Aggregation and Logic-Representation Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08514)
*Minhan Cho,Jimin Kweon*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文独立复现了两种提升大语言模型推理可靠性的方法（RPC和LCF），并在多个新任务领域和模型上进行压力测试。结果显示：RPC在作者发布的推理路径上能精确复现原始结果，但在四个新领域上相对自洽性（self-consistency）的优势均不显著；LCF的逻辑有效性方向虽然存在但很弱，其唯一正面效果也不显著，反而在另外两个模型上显著降低了性能指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两种方法（RPC和LCF）的原始评估均由各自作者自行完成，从未经过独立复现或跨模型、跨领域的压力测试；且LCF没有公开代码。鉴于这些方法声称能提升LLM推理的可靠性，独立验证其声明的可靠性对领域至关重要。

Method: 研究者独立复现了RPC（推理时聚合token概率和自洽性）和LCF（训练投影器将隐藏状态拆分为"内容"和"逻辑"部分，并将逻辑部分编辑到有效区域）两种方法。他们将两种方法扩展到四个新任务领域：text-to-SQL、法律抽取、谬误识别和判例分级。RPC使用了四种新任务领域配Qwen3-8B，LCF使用了四种7-8B模型。研究者重跑了RPC已发布的路径聚合，重新实现了LCF的投影器、对比学习和干预管线，并直接探测了LCF的表示。

Result: RPC在作者发布的推理路径上精确复现了原始结果网格；在四个新领域中，RPC相对自洽性的优势从不显著（平局或小幅混合差异，配对p≥0.28）；在BIRD领域（唯一变动预算的领域），优势随K增长如预期般增大，但最大差距（K=32时+2.5准确率，p=0.16）在将样本扩大至n=200后反转至-0.25。LCF的逻辑有效性方向真实但较弱（最佳子层0.82可分性对比语义属性对照的0.95）；其唯一正面效果（Qwen3的ΔProb）不显著（p=0.56），而在另外三个模型中的两个上显著降低了ΔProb。

Conclusion: 两种方法的原始可靠性声明均未能在独立复现和跨域跨模型压力测试中得到强有力支持。RPC的优势在新领域不显著且在小样本上不稳定；LCF的逻辑编辑效果微弱且在多数模型上有副作用。建议领域内对这些方法的有效性保持审慎，需要更大规模、更稳健的评估来验证其实际价值。

Abstract: We independently reproduce two recent methods for making large language model (LLM) reasoning more reliable, and stress-test them across domains and models (RPC across four new task domains with Qwen3-8B, LCF across four 7-8B models). The first, RPC, aggregates token probabilities and self-consistency at inference; the second, LCF, trains projectors that split hidden states into "content" and "logic" and edits the logic part toward a valid region. Validating such reliability claims matters because the original evaluations are run by each method's own authors and were never independently reproduced or stress-tested across models and domains, and LCF shipped no public code. We re-run RPC's published-path aggregation and re-implement LCF's projector, contrastive, and intervention pipeline, then extend both to text-to-SQL, legal extraction, fallacy identification, and precedent grading, and probe LCF's representation directly. RPC reproduces the original grid exactly on the authors' released reasoning paths; on four new domains its edge over self-consistency is never significant (ties or small mixed differences, paired p >= 0.28), and on BIRD, the one domain where we vary the budget, the edge grows with K as predicted but its largest gap (+2.5 accuracy at K=32, p=0.16) reverses to -0.25 when we enlarge the sample to n=200. LCF's logic-validity direction is real but weak (0.82 separability at the single best sub-layer versus 0.95 for a semantic-attribute control); its one positive effect (Qwen3 $Δ$Prob) is not significant (p=0.56), while it significantly reduces $Δ$Prob on two of the other three models.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [175] [Predict-then-Optimize Framework for Public Transport Line Redesign under Fluctuating Traffic Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11405)
*Zihao Guo,Andrea Araldo,Faycal Touzout,Mounim El-Yacoubi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种预测-优化范式下的公共交通网络主动重构方法，利用NSGA-III算法基于交通预测对PT线路进行结构性重新设计，在保持高Jaccard边重叠（超85%）的前提下，应对高频交通波动带来的性能衰退。在Mandl基准和北京大规模网络上验证，平均出行时间最多可改善25.8%，且保持完整的OD连通性，优于传统的跳站等反应式干预策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统公共交通线路设计仅针对额定（名义）交通条件进行优化，实际运行中交通波动会导致性能显著恶化。现有适应机制（如跳站）多为反应式干预，在面对大规模或周期性交通波动时效果不足，增量式调整难以应对，需要探索更深层的结构性重新设计来保持系统性能。

Method: 采用"先预测后优化"（predict-then-optimize）范式：首先利用扩散卷积循环神经网络（DCRNN）对交通状况进行预测，并构建预测误差的统计模型以评估预测不准确性；然后使用非支配排序遗传算法III（NSGA-III）对PT线路进行主动重新配置；通过施加高Jaccard边重叠约束确保运营可行性和限制结构变动幅度。

Result: 在Mandl基准网络和北京大规模网络上的计算结果表明：受控的PT线路重新设计在高交通波动下能带来显著的用户侧性能提升和运营成本降低，同时限制拓扑变化。北京网络中高变异性场景下平均出行时间最多改善25.8%，同时保持85%以上的线路重叠；与跳站基线相比（该基线会破坏许多OD对的连通性），本方法保持了完整的OD连通性。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持从静态规划向持续、自适应的PT网络设计转变。结构性重新设计相比反应式干预在高交通波动场景下更能有效保持用户和运营者性能，为公共交通网络的主动适应性规划提供了新的方法论依据。

Abstract: Public Transport (PT) lines are traditionally designed to optimize performance under nominal traffic conditions. In practice, operating conditions frequently deviate from nominal ones, leading to substantial performance deterioration. Existing adaptation mechanisms typically rely on reactive interventions, such as stop-skipping, which are insufficient under large or recurrent traffic fluctuations. In such contexts, incremental adjustments may not suffice. This paper evaluates the potential of deeper structural redesigns to preserve performance.
  We propose a method to proactively redesign appropriate parts of PT networks under high traffic fluctuations that would otherwise deteriorate operator and user performance. We adopt a predict-then-optimize paradigm in which PT lines are reconfigured based on traffic forecasts using the Non-dominated Sorting Genetic Algorithm III (NSGA-III).
  To ensure operational feasibility and avoid excessive structural changes, we enforce high Jaccard edge overlap between the original and redesigned networks. To assess prediction inaccuracies, we construct a statistical model of errors from a well-established deep learning predictor, the Diffusion Convolutional Recurrent Neural Network, trained on real-world data.
  Computational results on Mandl's benchmark and the large-scale Beijing network show that controlled PT line redesign yields substantial user-centric performance gains and operational cost reductions under high traffic fluctuations while limiting topological changes. On the Beijing network under high variability, average travel time improves by up to 25.8% while preserving over 85% line overlap. Unlike stop-skipping baselines, which break connectivity for many OD pairs, our redesign preserves full OD connectivity. These results support a shift from static planning toward continuous and adaptive PT network design.

</details>


### [176] [Convex-Concave Interpolation and Application of PEP to Bilinear-Coupled Saddle-Point Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11412)
*Valery Krivchenko,Alexander Gasnikov,Dmitry Kovalev*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了凸-凹函数、双线性函数以及具有双线性耦合的复合函数的插值条件，并将性能估计问题（PEP）框架扩展到复合鞍点问题的一阶方法上，以精确重构最坏情况性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 性能估计问题（PEP）通过优化问题来精确求解算法的最坏情况性能，但其可解形式需要满足必要的插值条件。此前插值条件主要针对凸或光滑凸函数，而鞍点问题中的凸-凹函数、双线性函数等尚缺乏相应的插值条件，限制了PEP在鞍点问题分析中的应用。

Method: 本文首先推导了三类函数的插值条件：(1) 凸-凹函数的插值条件；(2) 双线性函数的插值条件；(3) 具有双线性耦合的复合函数的插值条件。这些条件是PEP问题可求解的必要且充分条件。基于这些插值条件，作者进一步构建了针对复合鞍点问题的一阶方法的PEP框架。

Result: 成功给出凸-凹函数、双线性函数以及带双线性耦合的复合函数的插值条件，并据此构造了复合鞍点问题一阶方法的PEP，使得该类算法的最坏情况性能可以被精确求解。

Conclusion: 本文扩展了PEP方法的适用范围，使其能够处理凸-凹和双线性结构的鞍点优化问题，为一阶方法的收敛性分析提供了精确的工具。

Abstract: The Performance estimation problem (PEP) approach reformulates finding the exact worst-case performance of an algorithm as the solution to an optimization problem. Tractable formulation of the problem requires necessary and sufficient interpolation conditions. We present the interpolation conditions for convex--concave functions, bilinear functions, and composite functions with bilinear coupling. We also construct PEP for first-order methods for composite saddle-point problem.

</details>


### [177] [Tight Nonasymptotic Local Convergence of Sinkhorn-Knopp](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11760)
*Wenzhi Gao,Zhaonan Qu,Yinyu Ye,Madeleine Odell*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了矩阵缩放问题中的Sinkhorn-Knopp（SK）算法，首次提供了与现有渐近Jacobian论证速率匹配的非渐近局部线性收敛分析。在连通性条件下证明SK是双随机矩阵缩放的多项式时间算法，展示了SK的局部次优性并提供加速变体，并将稠密矩阵上一阶矩阵缩放算法的复杂度从O(n^{7/3}/ε^{2/3})改进到O(n^{9/4}/√ε)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管关于SK算法及其变体的全局收敛性已有大量文献，但其局部线性收敛行为仍缺乏深入理解。作者旨在填补这一理论空白，提供与现有渐近Jacobian论证结果匹配的非渐近局部分析。

Method: 首次对SK算法进行非渐近局部收敛分析，建立与渐近Jacobian方法匹配的收敛速率。利用所开发的分析工具展示SK的局部次优性，并提出加速变体。对于稠密矩阵，通过改进一阶矩阵缩放算法实现在低精度下的复杂度改进。

Result: 在特定连通性条件下，证明了SK算法是双随机矩阵缩放的多项式时间算法；证明了SK存在局部次优性并给出了加速方案；将稠密情形下的复杂度从O(n^{7/3}/ε^{2/3})改进为O(n^{9/4}/√ε)。

Conclusion: 本文为SK算法提供了首个非渐近的局部收敛分析框架，填补了局部收敛理论空白，同时通过复杂度改进展示了理论工具的实际价值。

Abstract: We revisit the Sinkhorn-Knopp (SK) algorithm for the matrix scaling problem. Despite extensive literature on the global convergence of SK and its variants, its local linear convergence behavior remains less understood. We address this gap by providing the first nonasymptotic local analysis of SK that matches the rate obtained from existing asymptotic Jacobian-based arguments. We show that under certain connectivity conditions, SK is a polynomial-time algorithm for doubly stochastic matrix scaling. With the developed tools, we showcase the local suboptimality of SK and provide accelerated variants. Finally, for dense matrices, we improve the complexity of existing first-order matrix scaling algorithms from $O(\tfrac{n^{7/3}}{\varepsilon^{2/3}})$ to $O(\tfrac{n^{9/4}}{\sqrt{\varepsilon}})$.

</details>


### [178] [A Wolfe-Type Spectral Conjugate Gradient Method for Nonsmooth Convex Optimization Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11689)
*Jauny,Gourav Kumar*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种基于Moreau-Yosida正则化的Wolfe型谱共轭梯度方法，用于非光滑凸优化，结合安全保障的谱参数与Dai-Kou型共轭参数及兼容非精确梯度的Wolfe线搜索，建立了全局收敛性和强凸假设下的R-线性收敛率，在多个基准和大型问题上表现具有竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非光滑凸优化问题在众多应用领域广泛存在，而现有共轭梯度方法主要针对光滑问题设计，对非光滑问题的适用性和收敛性保障不足。Bundle方法虽然处理非光滑问题有效但计算成本较高。因此需要一种兼具效率、全局收敛性保障且适合大规模非光滑凸优化问题的新型方法。

Method: 该方法基于Moreau-Yosida正则化将非光滑目标函数转换为具有Lipschitz梯度的光滑函数，通过非精确梯度计算获得正则化梯度；采用带安全保障的谱参数（safeguarded spectral parameter）与Dai-Kou型共轭参数相结合构造搜索方向；使用与正则化产生的非精确梯度相兼容的Wolfe型线搜索来确定步长；在强凸假设下分析收敛率。

Result: 在标准非光滑优化基准问题和大规模问题上进行了数值实验，与多种现有共轭梯度方法和Bundle型方法进行了对比。实验结果表明所提方法在总体上具有竞争力，在大多数测试问题上达到或优于现有方法的性能，同时识别并讨论了当前实现的一些具体局限性。

Conclusion: 该Wolfe型谱共轭梯度方法为非光滑凸优化提供了一种有效且可证的求解方案，兼具收敛性理论保障和实际数值竞争力，可作为大规模非光滑优化中Bundle方法的可行替代，但当前实现仍存在一些待改进之处。

Abstract: This paper proposes a Wolfe-type spectral conjugate gradient method for nonsmooth convex optimization, built on the Moreau-Yosida regularization of the objective function. The method combines a safeguarded spectral parameter with a Dai-Kou-type conjugate parameter, and uses a Wolfe-type line search compatible with the inexact gradients that the regularization produces. We establish global convergence of the method, together with an R-linear convergence rate under an additional strong-convexity assumption. The method is evaluated on standard nonsmooth optimization benchmarks and on large-scale problems, and compared against several existing conjugate gradient and bundle-type methods. The results show that the proposed method performs competitively overall, matching or outperforming existing methods on most problems tested, while a few specific limitations of the current implementation are also identified and discussed

</details>


### [179] [Data-Driven optimal control via Koopman operators and Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11808)
*Guuyuan Chen,Gaosheng Zhu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种数据驱动的稳定流形（DD-SM）方法，将Koopman算子表示学习与Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman（HJB）方程的几何稳定流形方法相结合，无需系统动力学先验知识即可从原始轨迹数据中实现端到端的最优反馈控制综合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统HJB方程的最优反馈控制求解面临维度灾难且需要精确的系统动力学模型，而实际系统中动力学未知或难以建模，亟需一种能够直接从数据学习的端到端最优控制方法。

Method: 1) 构建统一对称子空间分解（SSD）和扩展动态模式分解（EDMD）框架下的增广控制系统，联合逼近漂移场、控制矩阵及其空间导数；2) 推导不变和非不变字典空间的概率有限样本误差界，保证HJB方程近似特征系统的准确性；3) 通过Lyapunov-Perron算子和ODE扰动分析证明数据驱动稳定流形的半全局误差随训练数据增加单调递减；4) 建立闭环指数稳定性并量化最优性间隙；5) 开发包含自适应数据生成和深度神经网络逼近的高效算法流水线。

Result: 该方法在改进的van der Pol振荡器上的实验验证了其有效性，控制信号输出时间在1毫秒以内，实现了实时最优反馈控制。

Conclusion: 数据驱动的稳定流形方法能够在不依赖系统动力学先验知识的条件下，通过理论保证的误差界和稳定性分析，实现高效、可证明稳定的最优反馈控制综合。

Abstract: This paper presents a data-driven stable manifold (DD-SM) method, which integrates Koopman operator representation learning with the geometric stable manifold approach to Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman (HJB) equations, enabling end-to-end optimal feedback control synthesis from raw trajectory data without prior knowledge of system dynamics.
  We construct an augmented control system under a unified symmetric subspace decomposition (SSD) and extended dynamic mode decomposition (EDMD) framework for joint approximation of the drift field, control matrix and their spatial derivatives, and derive probabilistic finite-sample error bounds for invariant and non-invariant dictionary spaces to yield a provably accurate approximate characteristic system of HJB equation. Via Lyapunov-Perron operator and ODE perturbation analysis, we prove the data-driven stable manifold achieves monotonically decreasing semi-global error with growing training data. We further establish closed-loop exponential stability and quantify the optimality gap, both tightenable by refining model accuracy. An efficient algorithm pipeline with adaptive data generation and deep neural approximation is developed, outputting control signals within 1 millisecond. Experiments on a modified van der Pol oscillator verify the effectiveness of our method.

</details>


### [180] [Joint Identifiability and Conditioning in Finite-Horizon Continuous-Time Inverse LQR with Unknown Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11932)
*Meiling Yu,Yuan-Hua Ni,Lei Jiang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究有限时域连续时间逆LQR问题，从闭环状态-输入轨迹中联合恢复未知系统矩阵(A,B)和二次型代价权重。通过有限时域引起的时间变化最优增益作为内源激励，提出三个可计算的调节性指标来量化联合可辨识性，并开发了调节性感知的采样数据重建方法，实现封闭形式的系统矩阵恢复和凸半定规划下的代价权重辨识，同时建立了非渐近扰动界和一致性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逆最优控制（IOC）旨在从专家行为观测中推断潜在代价函数，但现有工作缺乏对系统矩阵和代价权重联合可辨识性的系统量化。特别是有限时域场景下，时间变化的最优增益为联合恢复提供了结构性机制，但这一机制的定量刻画和计算性条件尚不明确，且需要保证恢复的是真实系统而非仅行为等价的替代品。

Method: 本文提出三个可计算的调节性指标：(1)状态丰富度、(2)增益变化丰富度、(3)结构化代价算子的单射性，用于量化联合可辨识性条件。在此基础上开发了调节性感知的采样数据重建方法：首先从噪声测量中重建增益K(·)和闭环动力学矩阵A_c(·)，然后以闭式形式恢复(A,B)，最后通过凸半定规划辨识二次型代价权重。方法论还包含在次高斯观测噪声下的非渐近扰动界分析。

Result: 建立了联合可辨识性条件的充分性保证，证明在满足三个调节性指标的条件下可以恢复真实系统矩阵(A,B)和真实代价权重矩阵（而非仅行为等价的代理）。推导了非渐近扰动界并证明在次高斯噪声下完整重建方法的一致性，误差界显式依赖于所提出的调节性指标。数值实验验证了理论和调节性指标的诊断价值。

Conclusion: 有限时域的时变最优增益可以作为内源激励机制，使得系统矩阵和代价权重的联合恢复成为可能；三个调节性指标为联合可辨识性提供了可计算的条件；所提出的调节性感知重建方法在理论上具有可验证的准确性保证，在实际中具有诊断价值。

Abstract: Inverse Optimal Control (IOC) aims to infer the underlying cost functional of an agent from observations of its expert behavior. This paper studies the finite-horizon continuous-time inverse LQR problem from closed-loop state--input trajectories, where both the system matrices and the quadratic cost are unknown. The finite horizon induces a time-varying optimal gain, and this endogenous excitation serves as the structural mechanism that makes joint recovery possible. We quantify this mechanism through three computable conditioning indices, which measure state richness, gain-variation richness, and injectivity of a structured cost operator. Using these indices, we establish joint identifiability conditions for the inverse problem considered here. Crucially, these conditions guarantee recovery of the ground-truth system matrices $(A,B)$ and the true cost weighting matrices, rather than merely a behaviorally equivalent surrogate. We also develop a conditioning-aware sampled-data reconstruction method that reconstructs the gain $K(\cdot)$ and the closed-loop dynamics matrix $A_c(\cdot)$ from noisy measurements, recovers $(A,B)$ in closed form, and identifies the quadratic weights through a convex semidefinite program. We further establish the non-asymptotic perturbation bounds and the consistency of the full reconstruction method under sub-Gaussian observation noise, with explicit dependence on the same conditioning indices. Numerical experiments support the theory and illustrate the diagnostic value of the conditioning indices.

</details>


### [181] [Adaptive Bregman Proximal Stochastic Gradient with a Stabilized Barzilai--Borwein Step Size](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12009)
*Chenhan Jin,Shengze Xu,Binghui Xie,Kaiwen Zhou,Fan Jia,James Cheng,Tieyong Zeng*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出Ada-BPSG，一种无需线搜索的Bregman近端随机梯度方法，通过将SAGA梯度表与稳定的Barzilai-Borwein（BB）候选步长相结合，使用中位数聚合增量割线信息，解决原始BPSG方法对步长敏感的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bregman近端随机梯度（BPSG）方法将方差缩减组合优化扩展到欧氏光滑性无法良好刻画的目标函数几何结构上，但其性能对步长高度敏感：原始随机曲率估计可能剧烈波动，而线搜索方法则需要重复的近端计算，增加了计算成本。

Method: Ada-BPSG将SAGA梯度表与稳定的Barzilai-Borwein（BB）候选步长耦合。通过中位数整合增量割线信息，使近奇异的局部比率获得较小权重；同时采用显式保护机制将曲率估计转化为有界步长序列以保证收敛。理论分析从相对光滑性和分量级方差控制出发，在有限维赋范空间中建立收敛性保证。

Result: 证明了凸目标下O(n/K)的遍历收敛率、相对二次增长下重启线性收敛率，以及非凸情形下Bregman近端残差的O(1/K)界。在逻辑回归和稀疏非负矩阵分解实验中，Ada-BPSG在获得低目标值的同时，对初始步长的敏感性显著低于标准方差缩减基线方法，且无需线搜索。

Conclusion: Ada-BPSG通过自适应且稳定的步长选择机制，在保持Bregman近端方法处理非欧氏几何优势的同时，消除了对线搜索的依赖并显著降低了对初始步长的敏感性，为方差缩减组合优化提供了更鲁棒的实用方案。

Abstract: Bregman proximal stochastic gradient (BPSG) methods bring variance-reduced composite optimization to objectives whose geometry is poorly captured by Euclidean smoothness. Their performance, however, remains sensitive to the step size: raw stochastic curvature estimates can fluctuate sharply, whereas line searches add repeated proximal evaluations. We introduce Ada-BPSG, a line-search-free BPSG method that couples the SAGA gradient table with a stabilized Barzilai--Borwein (BB) candidate. A mediant aggregates incremental secant information so that nearly singular local ratios receive little weight, and an explicit safeguard translates the resulting curvature estimate into the bounded step-size sequence required for convergence. This design yields a direct analytical chain from relative smoothness and component-wise variance control to convergence in finite-dimensional normed spaces. We prove an $O(n/K)$ ergodic rate for convex objectives, a restarted linear rate under relative quadratic growth, and an $O(1/K)$ bound for a Bregman proximal residual in the nonconvex setting. On logistic regression and sparse nonnegative matrix factorization, Ada-BPSG combines low objective values with substantially less sensitivity to the initial step size than standard variance-reduced baselines, while avoiding line search.

</details>


### [182] [Direct Acceleration of Stochastic Root-Finding Without Variance Reduction and Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12043)
*TaeHo Yoon,Nicolas Loizou*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出双锚（dual-anchor）加速机制，可在随机根求解/不动点问题中避免锚基（Halpern型）方法因误差累积而失效的问题，实现无需方差缩减或批次增大的$O(\epsilon^{-3})$复杂度；对强单调算子可达$\widetilde{O}(\epsilon^{-2})$复杂度，接近下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 锚基（Halpern型）加速方法在确定性根求解问题中达到最优收敛率，但其加速效果无法直接迁移到随机设置——误差会累积，除非通过增批次或方差缩减技术强制减小方差。因此需要一个无需误差累积机制即可在随机设置中保持加速效果的新方法。

Method: 本文采用双锚（dual-anchor）机制替代传统锚基加速框架。该机制在迭代中维护两个锚点，天然具有误差抗累积性质，使得算法在固定批次大小下即可工作，无需方差缩减或双重循环递归正则化。算法针对随机根求解问题（期望意义下的cocoercive算子）和随机不动点问题（期望意义下的平方非扩张映射）进行设计，并为强单调算子场景提供适配版本。

Result: 在批次大小与迭代无关的条件下，实现$O(\epsilon^{-3})$的复杂度，且无需方差缩减或双重循环递归正则化。对于强单调算子，同一算法可获得更优的$\widetilde{O}(\epsilon^{-2})$复杂度，在$\epsilon$依赖方面几乎匹配信息论下界。

Conclusion: 双锚机制是随机设置下可行的加速方案，克服了锚基方法在随机场景中误差累积的根本限制；在强单调情形下接近最优复杂度下界，为随机求解器提供了理论保证上的效率提升。

Abstract: Acceleration for deterministic root-finding problems has been extensively studied in recent years; specifically, the anchor-based, or Halpern-type methods achieve optimal convergence rates with respect to the operator norm. However, acceleration via these methods does not directly carry over to stochastic setting due to accumulation of errors, unless one enforces diminishing variance via increasing batch sizes or variance reduction techniques. In this work, we show that another class of acceleration, namely the dual-anchor mechanism, extends to the stochastic setting without such error accumulation, in contrast to anchor-based algorithms. Consequently, we cleanly achieve $O(ε^{-3})$ complexity with iteration-independent batch size, without any variance reduction or double-loop recursive regularization, for stochastic root-finding (resp. fixed-point) problems with cocoercivity (resp. square-nonexpansivity) in expectation. For strongly monotone operators, the same algorithm attains a sharper $\widetilde{O} (ε^{-2})$ complexity, nearly matching the lower bound in terms of $ε$-dependence.

</details>


### [183] [A Multiscale Primal-Dual Interior-Point Relaxation Method for Large-Scale Optimal Transport Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12060)
*Shengyu Sun,Rui-Jin Zhang,Ruoyu Diao,Yu-Hong Dai*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种多尺度原始-对偶内点松弛方法（MSIPRM），用于解决大规模最优传输问题，通过多尺度外层框架和IPRM内层求解器的结合，能够高效处理全公式包含数万亿传输变量的超大规模问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模最优传输（OT）问题涉及海量传输变量，导致内存和计算成本过高，现有方法难以应对。亟需一种能同时降低内存需求并保持计算效率的求解策略。

Method: MSIPRM包含两层框架：(1) 多尺度外层框架逐步构建由粗到细的标准OT问题层，每层在自适应细化的活动集上求解问题，活动集基于上一层解支撑初始化；(2) IPRM作为内层稀疏子问题求解器，无需严格内点迭代，可直接利用前一子问题解进行热启动，并通过求解法方程组约化得到的Schur补系统高效获取牛顿方向。同时开发了基于IPRM近似解的有效支撑识别策略。

Result: 建立了Schur补矩阵的条件数估计，分析了算法的全局与局部收敛性质。数值实验表明该方法具有计算效率和可扩展性，优于现有求解器，可处理包含数万亿传输变量的大规模实例。

Conclusion: MSIPRM通过多尺度稀疏化与热启动策略，显著降低了大规模OT问题的内存和计算成本，为处理超大规模OT问题提供了一种可靠且高效的求解方案。

Abstract: Large-scale optimal transport (OT) problems involve a vast number of transport variables, leading to prohibitive memory and computational costs. To address these challenges, we propose a multiscale primal-dual interior-point relaxation method (MSIPRM). The multiscale outer framework constructs a hierarchy of standard OT problems at progressively finer levels. At each level, the OT problem is solved over a sequence of adaptively refined active sets initialized based on the solution support at the previous level. This yields a sequence of closely related sparse subproblems, thereby substantially reducing memory requirements. The primal-dual interior-point relaxation method (IPRM) serves as the inner solver for each sparse subproblem. Since IPRM does not require strictly interior iterates, it can readily use the solution of the previous subproblem as a warm start. To efficiently obtain the Newton direction, we solve a reduced Schur complement system derived from the normal equations. Furthermore, we develop an effective support-identification strategy based on the approximate solutions obtained by IPRM. We establish condition number estimates for the Schur complement matrices and analyze the global and local convergence properties of the algorithm. Numerical experiments on large-scale test problems demonstrate the computational efficiency and scalability of MSIPRM and show that it compares favorably with existing solvers. In particular, MSIPRM can handle instances whose full formulations contain trillions of transport variables.

</details>


### [184] [Competitive Analysis of Stock-based Thresholds via Prophet Inequalities in Continuous Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12073)
*Jiashuo Jiang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究连续时间下的K单位在线资源分配问题，提出基于库存的阈值策略（仅依赖剩余单位数而非时间），并针对多单位先知基准建立竞争分析框架，通过将问题归结为Poisson优化问题，证明了两阈值和三阈值策略分别达到0.6269和0.6816的竞争比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最优动态策略通常同时依赖剩余库存和时间，但在许多实际场景中，简单且实用的基于库存的阈值策略更具吸引力。然而，缺乏系统的方法来量化这类简化策略相对于最优先知基准的表现，尤其是当到达过程非齐次、估值分布随时间变化时。

Method: 作者提出一个新的竞争性分析框架：首先通过类型覆盖对偶（type-covering dual）重新表述问题；针对对偶的无限维且非凸特性，将连续时间对抗问题归约为一个Poisson优化问题PoisOPTRe_K；进而证明对手到达过程具有cutoff和late-filling结构，从而对两阈值情形得到精确的四参数表述，对一般阈值情形得到有限的嵌套区间表示。这些归约使竞争比可直接计算。

Result: 两阈值策略在K=2时达到竞争比0.6269，三阈值策略在K=3时达到竞争比0.6816。结果表明，忽略日历时间的简单基于库存的阈值策略能够实现强先知不等式保证。

Conclusion: 简单的基于库存的阈值策略虽不考虑日历时间，但在对抗性连续时间设置下仍能获得良好的先知不等式保证，且其竞争比可通过结构化归约精确计算，为实际部署提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We study a continuous-time $K$-unit online resource allocation problem with nonhomogeneous Poisson arrivals and time-varying valuation distributions. While the optimal dynamic policy generally depends on both the remaining inventory and the time left in the horizon, we focus on a simpler and practically appealing class of stock-based threshold policies, whose limited number of thresholds depend only on the number of units remaining. We evaluate these policies against the multi-unit prophet benchmark, which selects the best $K$ realized values in hindsight. Our main contribution is a new competitive-analysis framework for stock-based thresholds in continuous time. We first reformulate the problem through a type-covering dual. The central challenge for analyzing the dual is that the dual is both infinite-dimensional and non-convex: the adversary can choose time-varying arrival and valuation processes, while the policy performance depends nonlinearly on the stochastic inventory trajectory.
  We overcome these challenges by reducing the continuous-time adversarial problem to a Poisson optimization $PoisOPTRe_K$, and then proving sharp structural properties of its worst-case solutions. In particular, adversarial arrivals admit cutoff and late-filling structures, which yield an exact four-parameter formulation for two thresholds and a finite nested-interval representation for general thresholds. These reductions make the guarantees directly computable. For example, for two thresholds, we obtain a competitive ratio $0.6269$ for $K=2$; with three thresholds, we obtain the ratio $0.6816$ for $K=3$. In this way, we show that simple stock-based thresholds achieve strong prophet-inequality guarantees despite ignoring calendar time.

</details>


### [185] [Parallel Machine Scheduling with a Singler Server and Loading-Unloading Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12087)
*Keramat Hasani,Frank Werner*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究具有单一公共服务器负责装载和卸载操作的并行机调度问题，目标是最小化制造跨度。证明了当机器数为输入的一部分时该决策问题是强NP完全的，并分析了LS和LPT启发式算法的近似比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典公共服务器调度模型通常只考虑装载操作，而实际生产场景中服务器还需在每项作业处理结束后立即执行卸载操作。这种引入卸载操作的模型更贴近实际，但目前缺乏对其计算复杂度和启发式算法性能的理论分析。

Method: 首先通过归约证明决策问题的强NP完全性；然后针对固定机器数m，分析List Scheduling (LS)和Longest Processing Time (LPT)两种启发式算法的最坏情况性能比，推导出近似比的上界公式。

Result: 证明了问题在机器数为输入的一部分时是强NP完全的。LS算法在三台机器上近似比至多为5/2；对任意固定m>=3，LS的界随作业数增长趋近于4-3/m；LPT算法的有限实例比为3-2/m+(m-1)(m-2)/(mn)，随作业数增长趋近于3-2/m。

Conclusion: 带公共服务器装载与卸载操作的并行机调度问题比经典仅装载模型更难（强NP完全），且LS和LPT均具有常数近似比，其中LPT在大型实例中表现更优。

Abstract: This paper investigates a parallel machine scheduling problem featuring a single common server responsible for both loading and unloading operations. Each job consists of a unit-time loading operation, non-preemptive processing on one of \(m\) identical machines, and a unit-time unloading operation executed by the same server. The objective is to minimize the makespan. Unlike classical loading-only common-server models, our setting requires the server to handle an unloading operation immediately after a job's processing phase concludes. We prove that the corresponding decision problem is strongly NP-complete when the number of machines is given as part of the input. Furthermore, we analyse the worst-case performance of the List Scheduling (LS) and Longest Processing Time (LPT) heuristics. For three machines, we establish that Algorithm LS achieves an approximation ratio of at most \(5/2\). For an arbitrary fixed \(m \ge 3\), we show that the general LS bound approaches \(4-3/m\) as the number of jobs grows, whereas for Algorithm LPT, we prove the finite-instance ratio $3-\frac{2}{m}+\frac{(m-1)(m-2)}{mn}.$ Thus, for each fixed \(m\), the LPT bound approaches \(3-2/m\) as the number of jobs grows.

</details>


### [186] [On the Convergence Rate Lower Bound of Biochemical Computational Modules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12109)
*Yuzhen Fan,Chuanhou Gao,Shibo He,Jiming Chen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了生化反应网络中多输出物种计算模块收敛速率的简洁刻画，严格证明了收敛速率受Jacobian矩阵最大实部特征值的上界约束，并通过两个数值示例展示该理论下界如何影响收敛速率范围及其对反应速率常数的依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生化反应网络是分子计算的核心理论框架，但在有限时间的实际实现中，计算输出编码在物种浓度的极限稳态上，有限时间内的计算精度成为关键挑战。现有研究缺乏对多输出物种生化计算模块收敛速率的系统刻画。

Method: 本文提出了一种针对含多个输出物种的生化计算模块收敛速率的简洁表征方法，并严格证明该收敛速率受Jacobian矩阵特征值中实部最大（即最不负）的特征值所界定。

Result: 通过两个数值示例验证了理论下界如何约束收敛速率范围，揭示了收敛速率对反应速率常数的依赖性，为生化计算速度的系统评估提供了定量框架。

Conclusion: 该公式化方法能够实现生化计算速度的系统性评估，并为构建高精度、误差可控的生化计算模块提供了实用的设计度量标准。

Abstract: Biochemical reaction networks have become a central theoretical framework for implementing molecular computation. A key challenge is finite time computational accuracy, as computation outputs are encoded in limiting steady states (LSSs) of species concentrations while practical implementations operate for only finite time. This work proposes a concise characterization of convergence rate for biochemical computational modules with multiple output species, and rigorously establishes it as being bounded by the eigenvalue with largest (least negative) real part of the Jacobian matrix. Two numerical examples illustrate how the theoretical lower bound shapes the convergence rate range and reveals its dependence on reaction rate constants. This formulation enables systematic evaluation of biochemical computation speed and provides a practical design measure for constructing high-accuracy and error-controlled biochemical computational modules.

</details>


### [187] [The Advective Fisher-Rao Geometry of Deterministic Measure Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12111)
*Benjamin Gess,Johannes Müller*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的"平流Fisher-Rao度量"，用于由连续性方程控制的概率测度路径上的优化任务，并证明该度量能提供最优下降方向。该度量从三个不同视角自然涌现：路径测度上Fisher-Rao度量的零噪声极限、Freidlin-Wentzell大偏差率泛函的二阶变分期望值，以及动态最优输运中Benamou-Brenier作用泛函的Hessian。实验表明该度量在概率密度拟合中表现最优，而Gauss-Newton方法在速度场拟合中表现最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在概率测度路径上的优化问题中，需要一个合适的几何度量来引导下降方向。已有的Fisher-Rao度量在处理测度路径时存在局限，因此需要引入一种新的度量结构，能够自然地结合测度的几何性质与平流（输运）动力学信息，从而获得最优的优化性能。

Method: 作者定义了连续性方程约束下概率测度路径上的平流Fisher-Rao度量。该度量的合理性通过三个独立视角加以验证：(1) 路径测度上Fisher-Rao度量的重标定零噪声极限；(2) Freidlin-Wentzell大偏差率泛函二阶变分的期望值；(3) Benamou-Brenier动态最优输运作用泛函的Hessian。最后通过计算实验将基于该度量的优化方法与Gauss-Newton方法进行对比验证。

Result: 理论分析表明平流Fisher-Rao度量能够给出最优下降方向。计算实验显示，该度量在概率密度的最优拟合任务中表现优异，而Gauss-Newton方法则在速度场的最优拟合任务中占据优势。

Conclusion: 平流Fisher-Rao度量提供了一种融合测度几何与输运动力学的自然优化框架，在概率密度拟合场景下优于现有方法；同时，该工作揭示了不同优化目标（密度拟合与速度场拟合）对度量选择的不同需求，为未来的研究提供了新的方向。

Abstract: A novel advective Fisher-Rao metric is introduced for optimization tasks on paths of probability measures governed by the continuity equation. This metric is shown to lead to optimal descent directions.
  It is then shown that this metric arises naturally from three different perspectives: As the rescaled zero-noise limit of the Fisher-Rao metric on path measures, as the expected value of the second variation of the Freidlin--Wentzell large deviation rate functional, and as the Hessian of the Benamou--Brenier action functional from dynamic optimal transport.
  We supplement this geometric construction with computational experiments. Here, we demonstrate empirically that the advective Fisher-Rao metric yields the desired optimal fitting of probability densities, whereas the Gauss--Newton method yields optimal fitting of velocity fields.

</details>


### [188] [A Sharp Four-Layer Theorem for Integer-Occupied Slices of a Planar Disk-Slab](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12204)
*Xinyue Liu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究有理正定二次子水平集与闭条带在仿射格陪集上的交点，提出一个确定性的二维δ=3/4约简来选取原始对偶整数泛函和内椭圆，证明该泛函的整数水平最多占据四个，并通过显式有理实例证明该界是紧的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该论文旨在给出格点在椭圆（二次子水平集）与条带相交区域中的分布上界，通过约简和精确的Bernstein证书证明水平数最多为四，填补了此类格点几何估计中缺乏紧界的问题。

Method: 采用二维δ=3/4确定性约简选取原始对偶整数泛函和内椭圆；利用严格直径估计将反例归约到三块连续五个水平；通过最近整数不等式排除中心块；利用对称性和胞腔定位将两侧块约简为八个模式，其中四个由共享帽不等式处理，四个由含17,640个正系数（最小为625/2048）的精确四变量Bernstein证书处理。

Result: 证明了在Babai点位于椭圆外的分支中，盘-条带中的格点至多占据选定泛函的四个整数水平；构造出显式有理实例达到四，证明界是紧的。

Conclusion: 该结果给出了格点在椭圆-条带交集中沿特定泛函方向水平数的最优上界为四，并通过精确的计算机辅助证书（Bernstein证书）完成了严格的证明。

Abstract: Let a rational positive-definite quadratic sublevel set on a full-rank affine lattice coset be intersected with a closed slab. A deterministic two-dimensional delta=3/4 reduction selects a primitive-dual integer functional and an inner ellipse. In the branch where the corresponding Babai point lies outside that ellipse, the lattice points in the disk-slab occupy at most four integer levels of the selected functional. An explicit rational instance attains four, so the bound is sharp. A strict diameter estimate reduces any counterexample to three blocks of five consecutive levels, and a nearest-integer inequality excludes the central block. Symmetry and cell localization reduce the two side blocks to eight modes: four follow from a shared-cap inequality, and four from an exact four-variable Bernstein certificate. The certificate contains 17,640 positive coefficients, with minimum 625/2048.

</details>
